### Latest working notes
- [Triage: query-card freshness/supersession rung (#3163) — what shipped, what remains (2026-08-06)](docs/notes/SELFQUERY-CARD-FRESHNESS-3163-TRIAGE-2026-08-06.md) — classifies #3163's horizon from repo evidence *before* further implementation, and supplies the doc card the issue's own dogfood query could not find. The advisory currency rung on a `fak feature query` card already shipped (`74ca356fd5` + `903dc2769c`): `FeatureCard.Freshness` carries `FRESH` / `SUPERSEDED_BY:<resolvable card name>` / `STALE`, with supersession derived purely from same-topic dated-note filename ordering (`freshnessByKey`) and staleness from a read-time `os.Stat` re-check against the *current* tree (`stalenessByKey`), `STALE` winning the merge and both scoped per-root on the cross-repo path. Fences hold — advisory only, never re-orders or drops a card, `Cards()` leaves it empty so `SummaryDigest` stays stable — under nine named tests. Pins the **two-freshness-axis** disambiguation the issue trips over (`fak index freshness` = index-vs-tree drift; this rung = per-card content currency). Records the close: the last residual — the human table dropping the rung — landed as `c92b3ba341` (#5803, a sixth column plus an exact-output render test), so #3163's "compute *and print* `SUPERSEDED_BY`" step is met on `--json`, MCP, **and** the operator table. Leftovers are the never-started `supersedes:` front-matter edge and `Resolved by <sha>` signals — the first still gated behind #3161's edge extractor, which never shipped despite #3161 being closed against `cae824a` (a skills/notes commit touching no `internal/selfquery` code); the push/INVALIDATE half is already carved out to #3326.
- [Managed-cache message-prefix ablation (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/MANAGED-CACHE-MESSAGE-PREFIX-ABLATION-2026-08-05.md) — #2186 ordered message-prefix 1h cache upgrade, head-only ablation, attribution split, and break-even decision.

- [LiveCodeBench smoke against an already-running GLM-5.2 serve, over the control bridge (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/LIVECODEBENCH-SMOKE-GPU-SERVER-2026-08-05.md) — the durable recipe for the remote-GPU-server smoke: the named verb (`cmd/livecodebench raw`, the unadjudicated arm) driven by `tools/livecodebench_smoke_gpu_server.sh` (never starts a serve; distinct rc per failure; `.done` sentinel) with the artifacts pulled back **as files**. Witnessed run: `SMOKE_PASS nonempty=2/3 gen_s=67`, both artifacts md5-verified. The empty third sample is explained honestly — `finish_reason=length` with `content_len=0` and `reasoning_len=1681`, i.e. a reasoning model spending its whole budget on the private chain — so the default became `LCB_MAX_TOKENS=2048` plus an `EMPTY_HINT`, and the **re-run from the committed script witnesses that default at `nonempty=3/3 gen_s=94`** rather than assuming it. Three transport traps recorded because they will bite any remote-drive task here: the hub **ignores `!send-file`** (use `-message-commands`), Slack's autolinker turns a bare URL into `<http://…>` which bash reads as a **redirection** (send a base64 carrier, never command text), and a single-readback `pull` **truncates silently past ~1.4 KB** (split remotely, reassemble, verify md5). Fence held: `result_claim_allowed=false` — generation smoke only, pass@1 still needs the official `lcb_runner` evaluator.
- [Windows Defender exclusions for the hot-clone git object DB (2026-07-17)](docs/notes/GITGATE-DEFENDER-EXCLUSIONS-HOT-CLONE-OBJECT-DB-2026-07-17.md) — the operator runbook for the environment half of #4602 (#5080): the exclusion set (narrow `.git/objects` vs broad clone root, the `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Fleet\worker-worktrees` root, the `git.exe` process), the exact elevated `Add-MpPreference` / `Remove-MpPreference` commands, and the honest trade-off per exclusion; the Defender contribution to the ~2-min cold-git stall is labeled **modeled** — the measured before/after is `not yet`.
- [Relay policy-table field spec (2026-07-16)](docs/notes/RELAY-POLICY-TABLE-SPEC-2026-07-16.md) — the data-only spec for the `[relay]` dos.toml policy table (#1888): the five operator knobs (`soft_mark`, `hard_ceiling`, `rotations_per_hour`, `min_progress`, `done_hook`), each with a purpose, a type + default, and a citation binding it to the shipped relay knob and reason token it drives (`ArmTriggers.SoftMark`; `CeilingPark`/`RELAY_PARKED_UNSAFE`; `RotationCap.MaxPerHour`/`RELAY_ROTATION_CAPPED`; `ArmHysteresis.MinSteps`; `NoProgressEscape`/`RELAY_NO_PROGRESS`; `DoneCheck`/`RELAY_GOAL_DONE`) — so the table shape binds to real code rather than a sketch, with zero-value-disables semantics preserved per field. The dos.toml wiring itself is explicitly out of scope (follow-on); this note is the shape only.
- [Concept study — Sentry-LLM/SentryLLM: the top-scoring idea-scout lead was a scaffold → a substance gate for the crawler (2026-08-08)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-SENTRYLLM-2026-08-08.md) — a `/scout-loop` → `/study-repo` pass over the **highest-scoring repo-shaped lead in the open triage queue** (#5596, score **75**; ~116 open `idea-scout` issues, next repo-shaped 58), read at pinned `@1ac6b81b` (MIT). **Zero borrows, and that is the finding**: the README advertises an SDK, 10 detection categories and OWASP LLM Top-10 coverage with `[x] Prompt injection detection (v1)` marked shipped, while the tree implements none of it — the entire runtime is `GET /healthz` → `{status:"ok"}` (`artifacts/api-server/src/routes/health.ts:6`, the only entry under `paths:` in `lib/api-spec/openapi.yaml:14`), `lib/db/src/schema/index.ts:20` is the unedited Drizzle template (`export {}`), `replit.md:1` still reads `# [Project name]`, a whole-tree grep for `injection|jailbreak|exfiltrat|threat` matches **0 source files**, `registry.npmjs.org/sentryllm` returns **404**, and `.github/workflows/ci.yml:28,37` runs `npm ci`/`npm test` in a pnpm workspace with no lockfile. Not README-only but **scaffold-only** — 81 `.ts`/`.tsx` files, ~7,000 lines, which is the harder case for a size filter. The durable output is a defect in fak's own crawler: `tools/idea_scout.py:322-396` (`score_candidate()`) sums six terms — term hits, recency, stars, HN points, push-freshness, star velocity — with **no substance term**, and neither fetcher (`tools/idea_scout.py:824`, `:836`) requests a size field, so a hollow repo rode `very fresh` + `trending (34★/day)` to 75 (0 points from stars) while `NVIDIA/kvpress` (#5328, 1142 stars, real kernels) scored **30**. A fix must also widen the three-key `extra` allow-list at `tools/idea_scout.py:457-461` (else it is a silent no-op) and move the Go port with it (`internal/ideascout/fetch.go:57,67`, cross-pinned via `internal/ideascout/testdata/source_corpus.json`). Witnessed **ABSENT-on-axis** (two `fak feature query` phrasings + a raw `Grep` over `tools/ internal/ cmd/ docs/`; false-ABSENT control: `idea-scout`/`idea_scout` match 85× in the same 1.05 MB dump) → filed **#5891**, a repo-**size** admission floor (`size`, a free key on the existing `gh search repos` call — *not* `diskUsage`, which is GraphQL-only and would have killed both lanes) rather than a score penalty, which the six-summand arithmetic makes unsatisfiable. Pre-file precision, adjacent to the post-file triage-closure family #4072–#4076 under #3947. Lead #5596 closed *not planned* with the verdict. Honest limit: repo size is a proxy, not a substance oracle — the floor should catch scaffolds, not rank real repos.
- [Concept study — rtk (Rust Token Killer: hook-time tool-output compressor) → witnessed borrows for fak (2026-07-16)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-RTK-2026-07-16.md) — a `study-repo` pass over rtk (`@5d32d073`, Apache-2.0, all `inspire`/clean-room Go). Decisive tension: rtk and fak both intercept an oversized tool_result but recover oppositely — rtk is **lossy-by-default with human-only `tail -n +N` recovery**, fak is **lossless-or-opaque with model-callable `fak_context_restore`** — so fak can adopt rtk's lossy per-tool distillation *with a stronger safety net than rtk has*. The gap is real because fak built the seam and left it unrouted: `internal/headroom/headroom.go:41` documents `Input.Tool` as "a router hint" and **no compressor reads it** (`native.go:33` ignores it; every `switch .Tool` site classifies for security/verb, never shapes output). 3 witnessed **PARTIAL** filed (all `inspire`): **#5011** tool-shape-aware line distiller behind the `Compressor` seam (drop known-noise lines, keep failures inline via rtk's `match_output.unless` guard) — the rule-based complement to the **#3204** LLMLingua-2 token-level plugin on the same seam; **#5012** per-tool output-volume **opportunity** lens over sessions (rtk `discover`; `internal/sessionaudit` has the spine but no per-tool attribution); **#5014** deterministic **error→correction pair** miner (rtk `learn`; `behavior.go` `RepeatFailures` sees the error side, `nightrun/learningnudge.go` gates a scalar, neither extracts the pair). **2 DIVERGENT** recorded not filed: rtk's command-rewrite permission model (fak adjudicates in-loop, doesn't rewrite) and its filter-file SHA-256 trust gate (fak's #5011 scope is built-in filters only — the trust gate is the named prerequisite *inside* #5011 for the day user-supplied filters land).

The **residual** — a durable SQLite board + atomic-claim primitive — is *not* landed here: it is blocked on #2928's own `unknown — needs operator input` acceptance gate (board home package + test name), lives in the **cmd**/dispatch lane not docs (its `Likely files` are `cmd/fak/dispatch_*.go`), and its atomic-claim need is largely served by `dos_arbitrate` leases. Stream kept `needs-triage` (mixed horizon: shipped `gen/now` core + a `gen/next` durable-board foundation awaiting an operator schema decision).

- [CPU inference scaling: box-873af63b (32c/253GiB, gpu=none) — 5 axes + gemma4 #4274 (2026-07-11)](docs/notes/CPU-INFERENCE-SCALING-BOX873AF63B-2026-07-11.md) — an overnight fak-native pure-Go CPU characterization via `modelbench`, every tok/s read straight from an artifact field (`decode.tok_per_sec`/`prefill[].tok_per_sec`), not narrated. **Five OBSERVED scaling axes** (Qwen2.5 {1.5B,3B,7B}-Q8, Qwen3.6-27B-Q4_K): **(A)** decode vs model size (clean single-experiment @24c) = **16.4/8.9/4.0/1.05 tok/s** for 1.5B/3B/7B/27B — inversely with active bytes/token (bandwidth-bound stream). ⚠️ **Methodology finding:** the first pass benchmarked all five models *concurrently* and under-measured them **2.04×** (1.5B decode) to **8.3×** (27B prefill) — worst for the small bandwidth-bound models (decode) and the large core-bound one (prefill); every number here is a single-experiment re-run; **(B)** decode vs core count on the 7B **peaks at 24 workers (3.96) and REGRESSES to 3.54 @32c** (all-cores oversubscription starves the memory controller), while prefill(256) **saturates by 24c** (17.6→115.3→116.3 tok/s @2/24/32c) → 24 workers the sweet spot, `FAK_WORKERS=32` a decode pessimization; **(C)** prefill vs context **119→75 tok/s @P=256→2048** (sub-linear O(n²) attention over a still-dominant FFN); **(D)** decode is **depth-stable** (3.49 @256 steps vs 3.54 @64, −1.4%); **(E)** the decode sweet-spot is **U-shaped in model size, not monotonic** — both the lightest (1.5B) *and* heaviest (27B) peak at **16** workers (tiny→coordination overhead; huge→saturates bandwidth with fewest cores), the middle (3B/7B) at 24; all four regress at 32c (1.5B −42%, 7B −11%). Prefill runs opposite: large models (7B/27B) keep scaling to 32c. **1 bug filed — #4274**: gemma4-coding Q4_K resident CPU forward **panics** (`tpApplyQKNormBand` `tensor_parallel_forward.go:237` asserts the scalar `cfg.HeadDim`, but gemma4 has per-layer `headDimForLayer` and the `-q4k` path never dispatches to `gemma4.go`), runtime-confirmed **not** TP-specific (`FAK_WORKERS=1` panics identically). Ledger rows `docs/nightrun/collected.jsonl` (`cpu-*-scaling`, `cpu-decode/prefill-*`); per-cell artifacts are box-local (`experiments/nightrun/*` gitignored) — this note + the ledger are the durable record.

Symptom is the verbatim premise of **#3624** (banner-claim-vs-wire) under epic **#3569**, so recorded as a **prevent-at-source enrichment comment** (mirror the AUTO structural checks in ON → forced-but-inert passive with a named reason), not a competing leaf. Adversarial verify sustained all four; all INSPIRE (Py→Go).

- [Operating the operator-questions — evidence-decide the harness's human-gates (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-AGENTIC-OPERATOR-QUESTIONS-2026-07-10.md) — inventories every operator-style interrupt the Claude Code harness and this repo's guard/kernel raise (plan approval, `AskUserQuestion`, tool-permission, confirm-before-irreversible) and applies the fak/DOS inversion: **re-attach the evidence, decide the decidable part from an oracle, and hand the human only the residue no witness can settle** — as a closed-vocabulary `OPERATOR_GATE` reason with a `fak complain` appeal channel, never as prose. Finding: the harness already delegates H2/H6/H7/H8 to the agent and the repo already evidence-operates **H4** (`repo_guard`) and **H5** (`REQUIRE_WITNESS` / `internal/adjudicator/reversibility.go`), so the genuine frontier is exactly **two** questions — **H1 plan approval** and **H3 clarify/choose-approach** — each decomposing into an evidence-decided majority (`dos arbitrate` disjointness, architest/gitgate/core-lock direction, reversibility scan, `dos verify` done-criterion) plus a small named human residue. Ships **no code**; scopes epic Q1–Q5 (plan-scope pre-adjudicator → residue router → H3 resolver → decision witness → heaviness fence). Every checkable citation was adversarially re-verified against the tree (40/42 clean first pass; fixed a fabricated token → `BLOCKED_BY_HUMAN`, the `[reasons]` count → **53**, and `dos recall` → the `dos_recall` MCP oracle / `fak recall` CLI). Parallel subsystem readers → **46 grounded candidates**, one dogfooded capability-witness each (`fak_feature_query`/`fak_capabilities`+grep): **40 PARTIAL, 2 ABSENT, 4 NO-WITNESS**. Decisive pattern is *"fak has the seam, not the mechanism"* — it owns capability-narrowing but not CLI-grammar attenuate-and-admit; owns diff-witnessed distrust but the reviewer emits no per-finding `in_diff`/`off_diff` anchor; owns the deterministic runtime gate but not the authoring-time NL→regex compile; its `OverlayCache` skill-dedup is *request-path-dead*; its self-modify floor is *polarity-blind* (narrowing trips it like widening). - [Concept study (CORRECTED): DFlash — block-diffusion speculative decoding (NVIDIA / Z Lab) → fak's pure kernel (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-DFLASH-2026-07-10.md) — source-graded read of **DFlash** (Z Lab; arXiv:2602.06036; NVIDIA-highlighted 2026-06), the single-pass **block-diffusion** drafter shipped into vLLM via **Speculators v0.5.0** as a config-only EAGLE-3-style swap (`"method": "dflash"`): it drafts a whole B-token block in **one** forward pass (vs EAGLE-3's autoregressive draft), conditioned on the target's hidden states, so higher K is ~free. Numbers graded by source — **paper** ~6× on Qwen3-8B (~2.5× over EAGLE-3); **vendor** up to 15× / 5.8× on Blackwell (**hardware-gated**). **Corrected fak lens** (retracts an earlier draft's "no code borrow / fak never touches a KV tensor" — both false): fak's **pure kernel** owns the KV cache and ships a PARTIAL, bit-exact token-level **verify+accept+rollback** substrate (`internal/model/verify.go:36` `VerifyForward`, `internal/polymodel/polymodel.go:410` `AcceptGreedy`, `internal/model/kvcache.go:94` `KVCache.Evict`) with the **draft source as the one open gap** (#3197 / #3078). DFlash is thus an **inspire-class CODE borrow** into `internal/model` + `internal/compute` — a single-pass block draft head filling that gap, an alternative to EAGLE-3 / GLM MTP self-speculation — gated on a masked multi-query GPU kernel (absent) and lifting the `verifyForwardBatchedOK` backend/quant gate. Status `not yet`; first checkable step is #3197's n-gram drafter behind `Session.Generate`.
- [Borrow scout: vLLM Dynamic Speculative Decoding (#32374) → fak (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/study-vllm-dynamic-sd-borrow-2026-07-10.md) — read the MERGED Dynamic-SD change (PR **#32374**, squash `4ef4492e`, 2026-06-14) — `k` draft tokens made a function of **batch size** (cost ∝ batch × depth; past a critical concurrency a fixed depth goes net-negative). Distilled into three separable techniques (**T1** validated sparse-range→dense O(1) `batch_size→K` lookup, total-by-construction; **T2** grade optimistic depth DOWN as load rises, K→0 fallback; **T3** depth as a per-call param) and witnessed each on-axis. One strong deferred lead recorded (couple a per-worker optimistic-depth knob — warm-KV staging #4133/#3779 — to measured fleet load), not filed (proposes a new coupling adjacent to the #1333/#4036 derived-ceiling family).

- [Bottleneck map (2026-07-09)](docs/notes/BOTTLENECK-MAP-2026-07-09.md) — fleet health CRITICAL, headline = crash-resume backlog (13 AUTO_RESUME + 13 SURFACE), but the resume watchdog is healthy (LIVE, 10-min tick, plan draining 40→15): the CRITICAL is transient account-pressure churn (3/8 accounts throttled, reset 3:50pm PT), not a broken recovery layer. Durable limiter if it persists past reset is `MaxPerTick=4` (~24 resumes/hr) vs the death rate — but do NOT raise the cap during a throttle window (STOPPED_LIMIT feedback loop onto the 5 healthy accounts). Decision: don't add workers/raise cap now; next durable loop is completing ready leaves. Next-pass done condition: after reset, `auto_resume` < 8 and plan < 10 with 0 throttled accounts.
- [Completion-time benchmarking next steps (GLM-5.2 + DeepSeek) (2026-07-09)](docs/notes/COMPLETION-TIME-BENCHMARK-NEXT-STEPS-2026-07-09.md) — the ranked, checkable path from fak's MODELED time-to-solution floor (`fak frontierswe describe --tts`, the SWE-bench 17.9× turn-tax) to the first MEASURED faster-completion-time point. States the honest attribution — the win is harness efficiency (KV-persistence re-prefill elimination, fused serving, in-process adjudication, fewer turns), not raw model speed — and keeps the load-bearing caveat visible: the turn-tax lever only bites where **fak owns the KV** (self-hosted GLM-5.2 / DeepSeek behind `fak serve`), not on the closed DeepSeek API where the provider owns it. Splits the critical path into GPU-free-now steps (`fak serve /metrics` route, C6 route shim, C12/C14 compare+TTS metric, DeepSWE adapter) and GPU/Docker-gated steps (stand up endpoints, run raw-vs-fak, official grader, parity gate then ratio). Smallest honest win: one DeepSWE task, GLM-5.2 self-hosted, parity green, `T_fak/T_raw < 1`.

- [Borrow scout: Puppetmaster → fak (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/study-puppetmaster-borrow-scout-2026-07-10.md) — a `study-repo` pass over **Puppetmaster** (`professorpalmer/Puppetmaster`, MIT `9597e5b`, v1.18.0 *"AGNT-style all-1h Anthropic prompt cache for agentic BYOK"*), a **same-genre** 122-file Python BYOK proxy/harness — nearly every mechanism has a named fak counterpart, so the witness is unusually direct. ~11 candidates witnessed; almost all **PRESENT** and often more rigorous in fak (IFC-taint exfil floor vs static allowlist, `leaseref` fencing token + heartbeat + session cascade, elide/`savingsvector` plane, budgeted skill injection). **1 borrow filed** (`inspire`, Python→Go clean-room): **#3940** MMR-style redundancy suppression in `recall.Recall()` top-k selection (`mmr.py:60,106`), applied *within* fak's provenance-first ordering so a witnessed row is never demoted below a claim. **Correction embedded in the note:** an earlier draft called the all-1h cache a "reverse borrow / validation" — Puppetmaster omitting the `extended-cache-ttl-2025-04-11` beta as a latent bug fak was "strictly ahead" on. An adversarial re-verify (triggered by asking whether to file it upstream) **retracted that**: 1h caching is **GA** on the direct Anthropic API as of 2026 and the 2025 beta header is now a backwards-compat no-op, so Puppetmaster's inline header-free `ttl:"1h"` is correct current usage — no upstream bug, nothing filed against Puppetmaster. A fak-side "stale rationale" cleanup was then considered and **declined**: fak's own beta-union is safe either way (no-op if 1h is fully GA, load-bearing otherwise) and fail-open by design — not a defect, and removing a live gate on a WebSearch-only premise (the primary doc fetch was guard-refused) would repeat the same over-confidence against fak's own tree. One open lead (all-1h *moving-history* scope) deliberately **not** filed — a `cachevaluereport` measurement question, not vibe. Same genre as the [lmnr](docs/notes/study-lmnr-borrow-scout-2026-07-09.md) / [pxpipe](docs/notes/study-pxpipe-borrow-scout-2026-07-08.md) scouts.

One lead per pass: **CRAWL** the freshest signal → **SELECT** the highest-value repo-shaped lead → **STUDY** it via `/study-repo` → **WITNESS + FILE** each borrow via `/field-borrow` (PRESENT dropped, PARTIAL/ABSENT filed small) → **REGISTER** a dated note → **CADENCE** via `tools/register_scout_loop.ps1` (`FleetScoutLoop`, plan-by-default, `-Launch` the opt-in, no-DoS cap intact). - [Study-repo: AMD-AGI/Apex + EnvCommons/APEX-Agents → fak (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/APEX-STUDY-2026-07-10.md) — two pinned repos read through **the grader-as-attack-surface** lens: [AMD-AGI/Apex](https://github.com/AMD-AGI/Apex) @`33857b1` (**MIT**) — an agentic GPU-kernel optimizer that writes/grades/self-improves — and EnvCommons/APEX-Agents @`af080ff` (**no LICENSE → `inspire`-only**), an RL-env + rubric grader. Parallel deep-reader fan-out + completeness critic per repo. Decisive finding: Apex sits at **fak's own level** (an agent whose output is graded), and its grader defends a channel fak's witness stack does not — **a worker faking the checker green**. Most candidates dropped **PRESENT** with citations (artifact-as-oracle = `dos verify`/`commit-audit`; deceptive-403 auth-retry = `account_failover_test.go`; deterministic pinned-schema judge = `trajctl` `Temperature:0` + forced-tool-choice, stronger than EnvCommons' substring parse). Filed **2 epics + 4 leaves**: epic **#3921** (Apex) → **#3923** SOLUTION_GAMES_CHECKER antipattern detector (PARTIAL — registry spine present, no grader-gaming family), **#3924** pin-the-checker-bytes witness (ABSENT — fak hashes cache blobs, never the check's own bytes), **#3925** synonym+fuzzy `devindex` fallback (PARTIAL — pure `strings.Contains`, `internal/trigram` unused); epic **#3922** (EnvCommons) → **#3926** binary conjunctive rubric mode (PARTIAL — fak's rubric is soft `[0,1]`, extends #2544). Every source line citation re-verified against the pinned checkouts before filing. All `inspire` (Py/C++→Go, no bytes vendored).
- [Windows handles, consoles, and spawn storms: which ceilings are real (2026-07-08)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-windows-handles-terminal-limits-2026-07-08.md) — deep research + a live probe of the agent host, answering "why is the Windows handles/terminal thing such an issue, and why isn't it tunable?" Three unrelated failures were conflated under one name. **Kernel handles are not the ceiling**: 16,711,680 per *process*, hard-coded with no registry knob, against 243,735 system-wide on this box — the `find` incident was a leak whose real risk is paged-pool exhaustion (`ERROR_NO_SYSTEM_RESOURCES` 1450), not a count cap. The **pwsh `0xE9` FailFast (#2170) is a ConPTY-chain fragility bug, not a limit** — there is no cap on consoles/pseudoconsoles and no tunable; pwsh treats `GetConsoleScreenBufferInfo` as infallible and calls `Environment.FailFast()` when the console peer dies. The **low-usage stall (#3153) is `CreateProcess` cost** (>20× Linux) plus soft-fault contention on the PFN/working-set locks, invisible to CPU/RAM/disk meters by construction. The knobs everyone reaches for — `GDIProcessHandleQuota`, `USERProcessHandleQuota`, `SharedSection` — *are* tunable and *are* red herrings (headless processes never touching user32 don't consume them); `MaxUserPort` is deprecated. Witnessed: **`WindowsTerminal` is the top handle *and* thread holder on this host (17,582 / 902)** — the same process that froze dispatch on 2026-07-01 — so the "terminal handles thing" is app-level accretion in the emulator, not an OS refusal. Names two gaps in our own tooling (`host_stall_mitigations.ps1` uses only `-ExclusionProcess`, which per MS Learn does *not* stop Defender scanning the executable image on each spawn; `internal/stallscan` carries no handle axis) and names **Job Objects** (`ActiveProcessLimit` + `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`) as the unused kernel control surface for orphans and spawn admission. Ranked tune-now vs redesign vs do-not-touch.
- [Ghost slowdowns: a measured baseline and an ablation of where the churn comes from (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-GHOST-SLOWDOWN-BASELINE-ABLATION-2026-08-05.md) — the measurement half of the note above: what this box actually looks like when it is *not* stalling, and where the churn originates. Disk and RAM are conclusively ruled out (218 GB free, 94.9 % idle disk); the load is entirely in the fault/scheduler path. **Median 87.2 % of page faults are charged to no live process** (90.7 % on high-fault ticks) — the processes generating them are dead before any per-process counter is read. Ablation of 2,610 births over 101 s: **25.8 births/sec**, led by python dispatchers shelling out to `git` and small POSIX utilities (`conhost` at 27.5 % is a *shadow* of those spawns, not a source). Controlled injection prices one process creation at **8,166 page faults / 2,203 ctxsw / 12,981 syscalls** — floors — closing the arithmetic at **≥37 %** of the box's fault load. Two results changed the code: a PID-diff sampler caught **10 of 200** injected short-lived spawns (**20× undercount**, worst exactly where churn is worst — so the spawn axis is corroborating and the kernel-counted axes are primary), and a spawn *count* without its *window* is not comparable — the old threshold of 8 would have refused dispatch on **95 % of ticks of a healthy box**. Also indicts our own harness: the Stop-hook WIP checkpoint costs **~64,757 faults / 1.33 s per turn per session**, previously unbounded. Landed `5491894c2a95`; companion to [`internal/stallscan`](internal/stallscan/stallscan.go).

- [The claim-space IS the disaggregation primitive — one partition algebra, two planes (2026-07-08)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-CLAIM-SPACE-IS-THE-DISAGGREGATION-PRIMITIVE-2026-07-08.md) — the through-line that fak's control-plane claim-space (`dos arbitrate` / `laneadmit` / `regionadmit` / `dispatchorder.Plan`, claiming file trees by tree-disjointness) and its compute-plane disaggregation (`NativePDCluster` P/D, `ExpertParallelPlan`, `TPPlan`, `kvmmu` tiers, `DistComm`, claiming compute regions by data-disjointness) are **one partition algebra at two granularities**: `dispatchorder.Candidate{Lane,Tree,Mode}` read fresh is a resource request, and disaggregation is that primitive at a finer grain — scale-free from phone/IoT (one-at-a-time) to a hyperscaler mesh (100s–1000s of claimable units). The gap is that the two planes don't share an admission kernel; the fusion advantage (`polymodel` co-residency split at the tool-call-as-syscall seam) is the split point. Tagged `[SHIPPED]`/`[SEAM-ONLY]`/`[GAP]`, asserts no benchmark number. Tracked as **epic #3259** (children #3268–#3271).

- [Orphan-func scan — catching "built but never wired up" (2026-07-07)](docs/notes/ORPHAN-FUNC-SCAN-2026-07-07.md) — a pure tier-1 syntactic pass (`internal/orphanscan`) that flags unexported, receiver-less top-level funcs referenced nowhere in their package — the silent shape a dropped piece of work takes (Go never errors on an unused func). Precision-first (AST identifiers, not grep-over-comments; skips `main`/`init`/generated/`//go:linkname`/`//orphanscan:keep`). Dogfood over `cmd/fak`: 12/12 true positives, zero false positives, including a **confirmed dropped-wiring regression** — `guardMaybeRecoverCapCrash` (cap-crash auto-recovery inert; its auth twin is wired at `guard_child.go:1020`/`:1082`, it is wired nowhere) — and the orphaned spine provider `newGuardFleetProviderMaybeSpine`. The ablation surface (`fak ablate`) is clean/wired; nothing regressed there. **Follow-up (2026-08-06, #3167): the scan is now a gate** — `go test ./internal/orphanscan -run TestDogfoodRealRepo` asserts instead of logging, ratcheting against a reasoned `keep_allowlist.txt` baseline (currently 4) so only a *new* orphan reds the tree; a package with an unparseable file is declined rather than failed, and a stale entry logs rather than punishing whoever fixed it.

Explains why guarded sessions "compact when the 258K window looks light" (budget shown against window = ~37%), backs it with a fleet audit of ~2,967 local rollout files (trimodal compaction: ~973 fire ≥200K un-overridden, ~141 near the 96K budget, 32 premature <40K on stuck loops; 26% zero-tool turns; 15 guard-refusal-loop sessions), and ships the privacy-preserving monitor `tools/codex_turn_health.py` (exit-1 on HIGH_ZERO_TOOL_RATE / GUARD_REFUSAL_LOOPS / PREMATURE_COMPACTION). Best-practice defaults split safe-additive (the monitor) from review-gated (`tool_output_token_limit`, preamble suppression, refusal-alternative surfacing); keep `model_reasoning_effort` high.

- [Concept: anti-starvation dispatch aging — no ready unit waits forever (2026-07-11)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-DISPATCH-AGING-2026-07-11.md) — the missing feedback term in the issue→worker dispatch order: today `dispatchtick.OrderLaneCandidates` and `dispatchorder.Plan` rank ready work by base priority **absolutely** (age only breaks ties), so a light unit out-weighed by a steady drip of fresher high-priority arrivals is never picked — textbook priority starvation, which `dispatch-conservation` can only see *after the fact* as re-storm churn. `internal/dispatchaging` folds each unit's wait time into an **effective weight** (`base + aging_boost`, monotonic ⇒ eventually overtakes any fixed heavier tier) plus a **hard starvation deadline** (waited ≥ 6h ⇒ force-served this tick, bounding worst-case wait independent of the boost math); `Standing` is the closed `fresh|aging|starved` fairness verdict. **Shipped**: the pure fold + property tests (additive — zero `Params` reproduces the pre-aging order), and `fak dispatch-aging` as a read-only JSON diagnostic with a `--fail-on-starved` CI gate. **Gap**: the live picker doesn't yet consume the term (computed & reported, not enforced) — the first of five parked follow-ons, alongside a `dos.toml [dispatch.aging]` config, real `ReadySince` provenance, starvation observability, and cooldown-eligibility interaction.
- [Durable handling of guard false positives: match on structure, not mention (2026-07-02)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-GUARD-FALSE-POSITIVE-DURABILITY-2026-07-02.md) — the reversibility classifier ([#2376](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2376)) escalates commands that merely MENTION a trigger word (`git commit -m "...push"`, `grep "git push"`, `echo "rm -rf"`) and is simultaneously evadable by wrapping the same command in a script — one antipattern, matching on substring not call structure, wrong in both directions at once. Durable design in three layers: the structural-match doctrine (classify on the executed segment head / resolved target / egress host, never on payload mention; SQL and `dd` scans are the named payload-inspection whitelist), a growing false/true-positive corpus with a measured false-positive rate folded as a `guard-accuracy` scorecard, and a logged escape hatch (the shipped `29b98f26` in-band confirm recipe + a proposed `fak guard false-positive` ledger) so a routed-around escalation always leaves a trace. Names the second-order instance (the coarse `internal/adjudicator/**` hard-self glob firing on a non-self-grading preview classifier).

- [Multi-node concurrent development groundwork (2026-07-02)](docs/notes/MULTINODE-DEV-GROUNDWORK-2026-07-02.md) — design note for epic [#2254](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2254) (children #2297/#2299/#2300/#2302/#2304): how lease state, presence, and intent TRAVEL between hardware nodes, as three planes speaking one vocabulary — plane 0 git-carried `refs/fak/locks/*` converged by the new `fak leaseref sync` (shipped: push-then-fetch order load-bearing, fail-fast on push, no prune by design, deletions converge via TTL+reap), plane 1 the gateway dev-server (read endpoints, then single-arbiter fenced acquire — the only honest closure of the cross-machine atomicity gap), plane 2 GH-comment announce (evidence, never a lock). Plus the node-identity convention (`<node-id>/<session-id>`) and the target per-tick protocol (sync → reap → arbitrate → fenced acquire → fence-before-write → renew → publish).
- [The Slack control plane: one durable wire out, one authorized door in (2026-07-02)](docs/notes/SLACK-CONTROL-FOUNDATION-2026-07-02.md) — design note + SOTA survey for epic [#2259](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2259) (children #2261–#2268): what HEAD has (14 fire-and-forget outbound surfaces over two parallel clients; chatrelay-only inbound, deliberately not control), the dgx-bridge/fakrpc invariants recast as design rules (detach, out-of-band results, nonce-spool idempotency, single-writer drain, leak fence, structured capacity refusals), the verified 2025 Slack rate-limit terms (internal customer-built apps exempt — polling legal, strictly inferior to Socket Mode), outbound durability facts (no server-side idempotency exists; outbox + metadata-key + update-coalescing is the honest at-least-once contract), the HITL survey (Claude-in-Slack/Devin/Copilot/Codex/HumanLayer/LangGraph), the Go websocket landscape (`coder/websocket` consensus; hand-rolled Socket Mode ≈300 lines), and the chatops security fence: channel text is never instructions — closed verbs or refusal.
- [The tool process table: mastering long-running tool use (2026-07-02)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-TOOL-PROCESS-TABLE-2026-07-02.md) — the tool-call-granularity row the no-babysitting doctrine leaves open: fak disposes a call at admission and its payload at re-entry, but a background shell / monitor / polled job crosses the floor as uncorrelated events with no deadline, stall, orphan, or post-kill control. Ships the decision spine (`internal/toolproc` pure fold + `fak toolproc ps|sample`; closed verdicts TOOL_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED→kill, TOOL_HEARTBEAT_STALLED→probe, TOOL_ORPHANED→reap, TOOL_RESULT_AFTER_KILL→quarantine_result at out-of-tree reason codes 1040–1043) and maps the six enforcement seams (gateway supervisor, result-admission rung, MCP progress/cancel, harness hooks, policy runtime envelope, procguard binding) as labeled next steps.
- [Safe concurrency headroom on the agent host (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/SAFE-CONCURRENCY-HEADROOM-2026-07-01.md) — witnessed capacity audit of the one-box fleet: `host_cap`=16 (cores-bound) with 178 GB RAM free while the dispatcher sits at **0 live workers** because one PROTECTED foreign process (the operator's own terminal, 2,767 threads) trips `host_check()`'s any-flag fold into a fleet-wide REFUSE_HOST — a liveness bug, not a safety win. Ranks the safe raise-concurrency levers: the REFUSE_HOST carve-out (advisory for protected non-fleet flags), seats as the true multiplier (each seat safe by construction at one worker per account), static caps raised to the design intent so the adaptive gates govern, an elevated reaper for Session-0 zombie threads, measured per-worker charge constants, and the scaffolding (guard front door, ~3-spawn waves, lane leases, witnessed closes) that must scale with the count. Ceiling estimate: ~4–5 tonight, ~7 after 2026-07-04, hardware green to 16.
- [fak guard's share of cache value on real sessions — proven (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/FAK-GUARD-CACHE-VALUE-SHARE-2026-07-01.md) — the epic [#1844](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1844) headline split, measured from the durable ledgers: on 11 real `fak guard -- claude` sessions the provider prompt cache accounts for 153.1M token-equiv and fak's own mechanisms for **0 (0.0000%)** — the compaction-row absence is a witness, not a gap, because the same guard-exit writer emits both mechanisms; folding the `fak run` kernel sessions in, fak's witnessed slice is 3,441 tokens (**0.0022%**). `fak cachevalue report` now prints the pre-divided `fak_share` per period (refused with `-` when a period total is not positive) and carries `fak_share_pct` in the JSON, with scope fences (placement is identity on Claude Code, managed-cache TTL gated off, recorded rows only) and the #1844 levers that would move the number.
- [One binary, eleven orders of magnitude: the dynamic-range contract (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/DYNAMIC-RANGE-ONE-BINARY-2026-07-01.md) — doctrine note + epic [#2218](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2218) (children #2219–#2224) for fak's temporal dynamic range: one Go binary adjudicates a tool call in ~0.4–2.4 µs (`Decide` 362 ns/op) and orients a days-long `superloop walk tend` intent — the band ladder B0→B7 with witnessed anchors, the five one-binary wins (one closed refusal vocabulary across clocks, one admission structure, transport collapse as a repeatable move, one witness discipline, one budget-envelope shape), the risk register each win drags in (monoculture blast radius; version skew across running copies — witnessed: `fak version` prints "no VCS stamp"; fail-open vDSO budget coverage; backpressure that flows down but not up; cross-band causality per the SWE-bench empty-patch case; runtime-tail coupling), six laws, and the gap→ticket table (G1 vDSO budget row #2219, G2 provenance/skew witness #2220, G3 gate-pressure cap term #2221, G4 budget cascade #2222, G5 full-span trace + loaded p99 #2223, drive rung #2224). Keeps the gate-ladder fence: different clocks never multiply into one end-to-end number.
- [Meaningful control, one sidecar, automatic context: the three-plane split (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-CONTROL-SIDECAR-AUTOCTX-2026-07-01.md) — disambiguation + strategy for the operator's three-theme goal: one litmus test (*a knob belongs to the user iff it encodes intent the system cannot infer*) splits every knob into an INTENT plane the user meaningfully controls (epic [#2208](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2208), children #2210–#2212: knob census, `fak session pin`, envelope wall/spend/throughput routes), a LEGIBILITY plane — the sidecar/rollup view where the same session/account/lane/posture facts and shared items render identically across agents, surfaces, and hosts (epic [#2209](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2209), children #2213–#2216: cross-agent census, session-descriptor join schema, parity-witnessed pane, shared items live) — and the HOUSEKEEPING plane that stays zero-knob automatic under the existing #2198/#1844 programs. Two opposing ratchets (R1 #2199 down on housekeeping knobs; A1 #2210 down on route-less intent levers) keep the boundary honest; the operator's own example lands as INTENT = `fak session branch` (#1200) vs HOUSEKEEPING = routine carryover (#2198 R5/R7).
- [No babysitting: nobody watches a healthy fleet (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-NO-BABYSITTING-2026-07-01.md) — the attention doctrine that makes unattended operation a falsifiable product property, and the umbrella over the automatic-context (#2198), perpetual-sessions (#1860), and loop-kernel notes: babysitting is *polling*, and the kernel's job is to convert every poll into a rare, cheap, evidence-carrying interrupt. Decomposes supervision labor into seven watches (liveness, truth, safety, collision, budget, unblock, quality/goal-fit), argues which ones the improving model/harness slope retires vs **intensifies** (the trust asymmetry is structural; fan-out outruns constant attention; blast radius grows with delegated privilege; concurrency grows races; crashes are infra-shaped — the 2026-07-01 bottleneck map's recovery-layer CRITICAL is the datapoint), states seven laws (poll→interrupt; green means witnessed, not quiet; every interrupt is a typed packet; silence is bounded; autonomy is earned mechanically; recovery is the default path; the residual is human on purpose), defines the babysitting counter (touches per witnessed unit, silent hours, session MTTR, escalation handling time), and names rungs R1–R6 (counter → escalation packet → waiting-on-human queue → recovery drain witness → autonomy ratchet → unattended soak witness). Filed 2026-07-02 as epic [#2269](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2269) with labeled children #2270–#2277.
- [A surfaced choice is a triage, not a page (2026-07-07)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-CHOICE-IS-A-TRIAGE-2026-07-07.md) — the decision-side dual of the no-babysitting doctrine (#2269): before bounding how a human page behaves, ask whether the page was genuine at all. Almost every choice a fleet surfaces is fake — fold it honestly and it lands in one of four places: `TAKE_OBVIOUS` (one sane move an agent just takes), `FRESH_CONTEXT` (knowable but not obvious → a fresh context window at the top model tier), `FILE_TICKET` (real work too big for one context → a GH ticket), or `HUMAN_RESIDUAL` (a genuine policy/auth/release/priority/trust call — the only case that waits). The load-bearing inversion: the default is `FRESH_CONTEXT`, never `HUMAN_RESIDUAL`; a human decision must be *earned* by a signal naming authority a person holds, so the residual shrinks toward zero. Shipped: `internal/choicetriage` (tier-1, pure, closed `Disposition` vocab + deterministic `Triage`) and its first consumer `internal/operatorbrief` (every `Choice` now carries `Disposition`/`Resolve`/`NeedsHuman`; a missing-report page with a runnable fix becomes `TAKE_OBVIOUS`, a release go/no-go stays `HUMAN_RESIDUAL`). Filed as epic [#3136](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3136) with the worktype-unification rung [#3137](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3137).

- [Automatic context: nobody manages the window (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-AUTOMATIC-CONTEXT-2026-07-01.md) — concept note + epic [#2198](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/2198) (children #2199–#2206): the zero-knob doctrine that binds the managed-context (#1570), relay (#1860), cache-default (#1490), prompt-MMU (#751/#1258), and ctxplan (#844) programs to one falsifiable product property: *nobody — human or agent — manages the context window*. States the virtual-memory thesis (every manual `/compact`, hand-written handoff baton, "don't read that file," breakpoint, or TTL choice is a hand-written overlay), the two-sides-of-one-coin unification (residency in the window and warmth in the caches are projections of one address space, priced by **one ledger**, decided together), seven laws (one manager per wire; the window is a view; removal is placement; one coin; zero knobs on the default path; abstain honestly; every surface declares its context plan), a HEAD-verified wiring map (live pipeline vs dormant `syspromptmmu`/`memview`/`ctxresidency` vs gated-off vCache loop), the manual-overlay inventory (H1–H5 human, A1–A4 agent, plus server-side), a per-surface infusion table, and rungs R1–R8 (manual-overlay counter → one-ledger join → placement dual-write → context-plan-required gate → auto-envelope → retire the big-file warning → relay-by-default → the witnessing readout). Honest fences: bulk-closed managed-context children are design-shipped until #1918 witnesses the contract; WITNESSED never blended with OBSERVED.

Positions vs RouteLLM / Arch-Router-1.5B (same Qwen2.5-1.5B arch fak already runs), argues the residency case for a LOCAL scout (classify-first without pre-adjudication egress), and specs the binding: label-token logit scoring (closed-vocab by construction), ambiguity-gated invocation, a routebench scout arm, LoRA distillation from #600 telemetry. The binding itself is a named `not yet` (issue filed).

- [Native harness next steps vs Crush (2026-06-30)](docs/notes/NATIVE-HARNESS-NEXT-STEPS-VS-CRUSH-2026-06-30.md) — where fak's kernel-owned agent loop (#1315, children #1316-#1323, all built + diff-witnessed) stands against Crush, the Go reference agent at `C:\work\crush`. The decisive finding: Crush does **not** own its loop — the send→tool-calls→execute→repeat iteration lives inside `charm.land/fantasy.Agent.Stream`, and Crush plugs in via callbacks; `fantasy` is exactly the layer fak's `RunArm` + kernel syscall boundary replaces. The one open child is #1380 (the DoD witness run, Mac Metal / GPU only). Next GH steps, worst-regret-first: ship #1380, then borrow Crush's *host* seams — streaming native serve (filed #1837) and durable SQLite-class sessions (#1363/#1365) — while keeping the kernel seam Crush lacks.

- [Policy amendment classes: who may amend the guard floor, in which direction, through which channel (2026-07-17)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-POLICY-AMENDMENT-CLASSES-2026-07-17.md) — concept note for epic [#5170](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5170): the four amendment classes (FROZEN / RATCHET / GATED-WIDEN / SELF-AMENDABLE), the five amendment channels, the self / external-online / current-heaviest cross-product table, the invariants, and the `internal/amendclass` registry as the single source of truth — a knob's class is a capability (direction × channels), not a linear rank.
- [Intervention as a state operator: the physics-and-proof layer beneath next() (2026-07-17)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-INTERVENTION-AS-STATE-OPERATOR-2026-07-17.md) — epic [#5202](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5202): every shipped intervention (trajctl re-anchor nudge, sessionctl Next moves, doomloop NUDGE) is a token disturbance appended to an unchanged poisoned context, escalated on elapsed time; four lenses (control theory, Bayesian belief-state, actuator surface, causal verification) converge on the replacement — a **typed operation on agent state / action-space / dynamics**, routed by which latent corrupted (G/W/P), stamped with the evidence-rung it may assert at, carrying a witnessed acceptance predicate, escalating in **strength not time**, evaluated on an exact forked counterfactual. The physics-and-proof layer under `next()` [#4992](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/4992).
- [The look-ahead reset: speculative rollout + witnessed distillation + reset-with-lesson (2026-07-17)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-LOOKAHEAD-RESET-2026-07-17.md) — the highest-value intervention under [#5202](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5202) (children [#5204](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5204)–[#5210](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5210)): because the belief state is a copyable context prefix, **fork at a clean checkpoint → speculatively roll forward → witness at W2/W3 → distill a compact lesson → reset and re-render with the lesson folded in**. The rendered trajectory is poison-free yet carries the foresight, so it *dominates* rollback-alone (loses the info) and nudge-alone (keeps the poison) — proof via Blackwell decision-sufficiency + Howard VoI + Bertsekas rollout-improvement; the paired fork makes the instance-level counterfactual **exact**. Cheapest-first dogfood (P0 PreCompact mini-lookahead → P1 doomloop fork-confirm → P2 full drift reset) mapped to real fak seams.
- [Look-ahead reset dogfood tracker: P0 → P1 → P2 runbook + witnessed tracking (2026-07-17)](docs/notes/LOOKAHEAD-RESET-DOGFOOD-TRACKER-2026-07-17.md) — the operational tracker for [#5220](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5220) under epic [#5202](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5202): sequences the three dogfood protocols cheapest-first with their **witnessed done-conditions** — P0 [#5207](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5207) (≥1 rollout produced a W2+ lesson injected at SessionStart(compact), from ledger+transcript) → P1 [#5208](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5208) (≥K confirm rollouts + calibrate's measured K-window false-positive rate) → P2 (post-reset curve reaches its next W3 landing in fewer turns than the pre-drift span) — and owns the one-long-real-session evidence capture plus the 4-arm A/B fold ([#5210](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/5210)). Rows move from `not yet` only on witnessed evidence, never self-report.
- [Perpetual sessions: bounded-context relays instead of compaction (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-PERPETUAL-SESSIONS-2026-07-01.md) — concept note + GitHub epic [#1860](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1860) (children #1861-#1910) for running a goal in O(1) peak context: stop at a safe point, externalize every load-bearing fact to a durable store, hand a fresh session a small typed **baton** of pointers it re-verifies, and discard the transcript — explicitly *not* compaction. Extends the managed-context epic #1570 (`ctxplan`/`sessionreset`/`Recontinue`) with a pointer-only carryover policy, a fail-closed externalize gate, and perpetual-goal semantics (done-check, anti-thrash, no-progress escape).

- [GLM-5.2 full-GPU-resident on all 8 GPUs: the resident-fit benchmark answered (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/GLM52-8GPU-FULL-RESIDENT-SERVE-2026-07-01.md) — the resident-fit question the [expert-parallel note](docs/notes/GLM52-EXPERT-PARALLEL-MULTIGPU-2026-06-29.md) left open is now **measured** for the llama.cpp engine on the 8×A100-80GB GPU server: GLM-5.2 UD-Q4_K_M loads full-GPU-resident across **all 8 GPUs** (≈434.6 GiB VRAM, per-GPU residency witnessed), ≤6-minute load from local NVMe, and serves OpenAI `/v1` on `:8000` at **23.2 tok/s** single-stream decode (`print_timing`, 256-token runs ×2) — ~8.9× the 2.62 tok/s `--n-cpu-moe` cpu-offload wall. - [sm80 CUDA 13 issue #973 closure witness (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/SM80-CUDA-ISSUE-973-WITNESS-2026-06-29.md) — targeted datacenter GPU-class sm_80 CUDA 13.0 witness for the two #973 residuals: `TestCUDAGraphDecodeParity` and `TestCUDAQ4KBatchedMatMulApproxMatchesRef` both pass, with Q4_K batched cosine `1.00000000`; explicitly does not claim the full original `CUDA|HALDevice` umbrella, and leaves the separate f32 failures to #972.
- [Native-harness progress tracking (#1315) (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/native-harness-progress-tracking-1315.md) — the roll-up for [epic #1315](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1315), *fak owns the native agent loop end-to-end — the dispatch-edge program*. On the proxy path the external harness (Claude Code/codex) owns the turn loop and *consumes* tool calls outside fak; the native harness is fak *owning* dispatch (its own loop drives the turns, the kernel is the only tool path), the precondition for every "impossible on proxy" capability (vDSO tool-result materialization, before-consumption write barrier, speculative serve). Produced from an **8-surface, 90-claim survey** whose every SHIPPED/PARTIAL claim was re-checked by an adversarial pass against git + request-path reachability — **27 downgrades** on the one question that matters: *is there a live, non-test caller on the request path?* The honest one-liner: **the engine is owned, the loop is not (yet).** SHIPPED + live on the serve path: the native `/v1/messages` front door, in-kernel generation (`--gguf`), in-kernel tool-call render+lift+adjudication, fail-closed on malformed calls, vDSO read-serve, per-request session admission, the context planner, KV planned-elision, inbound quarantine, the `fak guard` proxy gate, the syscall boundary + A/B comparator. PARTIAL (built + tested, **zero live caller**): `RunArm` itself (only the `fak agent` benchmark + bench/ablate callers; the served path is single-shot `s.planner.Complete`), `WithSessionTable`/`gateTurn`, `drainSteer`, `WithRouteManifest`, `ApplyPace`, `WaitResume` warm-KV splice, in-loop streaming, the AgentDojo steward sweep, the multi-session scheduler, the intent conduit (#805), the loop-lifecycle ledger. NOT_YET (named gaps, keystones first): the **suspend-and-resume turn primitive** (`gateTurn` *terminates*, it does not suspend — the one net-new mechanism the canonical note names) and the **before-consumption write barrier** (SEAM-4 `abi/speculate.go` is committed `d859ec21` but `Speculator.Predict` has zero production callers), plus the consistency-level field on `ToolCall`, the native TUI/REPL (`fak chat`), and the system-prompt-MMU spine's first live caller. 8 ordered children #1316–#1323 (keystones #1316 a live `RunArm` caller, #1318 the suspend/resume primitive). Forward framing in [the vToolcall fork note](docs/notes/VTOOLCALL-FORK-AND-BESTEFFORT-2026-06-25.md); close children per [the epic close-out method](docs/notes/EPIC-CLOSEOUT-METHOD-2026-06-29.md) (a CLOSED box is a self-report, not a witness).
- [Epic close-out method — properly closing (or retiring) an epic (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/EPIC-CLOSEOUT-METHOD-2026-06-29.md) — the backlog is epic-heavy (57/171 open are epics); this is the repeatable method for closing one **honestly**. Guards the two failure modes — *false-close* (a child's `CLOSED` state / `- [x]` box is a self-report, not a witness; the syspromptmmu #1258 children were docs-closed while the code stayed UNBUILT) and *stale-open* (shipped epics that nobody re-checked). Three routes: **A COMPLETED** — every child closed AND the deliverable witnessed in the tracked tree (artifact on disk + claimed SHAs real + `dos commit-audit` OK/diff-witnessed + residual carved), closed with the #1147 house-style evidence comment (worked example: **#1116** closed 2026-06-29); **B NOT_PLANNED** — superseded/deduped, point to the survivor and fold framing there (example #1226→#1217; candidate clusters: serving #50/#637/#931, agent-OS #748/#749/#750/#1178/#1193); **C STANDING** — a "keep-the-scorecard-alive" perpetual loop (#710/#801/#1050/#1278) is retired from the deliverable count (split finite children out, drive the recurring part from a `/loop`, mark standing), never closed as done. Includes the read-only bulk close-state audit recipe and the anti-gaming laws.
- [The native-code doctrine (#1124) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/NATIVE-CODE-DOCTRINE-1124-2026-06-28.md) — fak's written rule for dropping below portable Go: Go-assembly (`.s`) is welcome on the model/compute path and is **not** cgo; `cgo`/`import "C"` is justified only for GPU device code behind an opt-in build tag; neither is ever allowed on the request/adjudication path. Plus the verified backlog of hot scalar kernels worth vectorizing (C1–C6) and the four places native code must never go.
- [Committing shared-clone WIP is an integration pass, not a freeze (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/SHARED-CLONE-INTEGRATION-PASS-2026-06-29.md) — runbook for an agent told to commit/clean up work in progress on the shared fak clone: land complete, self-contained, building units by explicit path; park the rest; gate the push with an archive-HEAD build — instead of mislabeling collective fleet WIP as untouchable peer work.
- [fak issue-backlog dispatch loop — handoff (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/DISPATCH-LOOP-HANDOFF-2026-06-29.md) — handoff from a session that shipped 26 issues via headless lane-disjoint workers: the paste-ready goal prompt, that session's laws, and a live backlog probe (the adversarial red-team pass was cut by a session limit, so the tooling sketches are proposals, not vetted specs).
- [GLM-5.2 multi-GPU: expert-parallel sharding + the resident-fit benchmark (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/GLM52-EXPERT-PARALLEL-MULTIGPU-2026-06-29.md) — the **expert-parallel (EP) MoE FFN decomposition** for GLM-5.2 is now host-proven on trunk (`internal/model/expert_parallel.go`, `f713391c`): partition the experts across ranks (`ExpertParallelPlan`, a named `TPPlan` over `[0,NumExperts)`), each rank computes only the picks it owns, one `AllReduceSum` of the per-rank `[H]` delta — the cheap MoE-native multi-GPU decomposition `forwardTPSupported()` fails closed on, and the one the overnight cpu-offload data points to (move the expert GEMM off the host onto idle GPUs). The gate triad runs with **no multi-GPU hardware** (`LocalCollective`): `EP(ranks=1)`==routed monolith **max\|Δ\|=0**, collective==rank-order ref **max\|Δ\|=0** (ranks 1/2/4/8), `EP(ranks=N)` cosine 1.0 (~3.7e-9 reassociation drift), + load-imbalance + fail-closed + a GLM shared-expert wrapper on a real `glm_moe_dsa` fixture. Makes the first **benchmark** answerable: the GPU server's **8×80GB = 640 GiB** VRAM (WITNESSED) fits GLM-5.2 UD-Q4_K_M (**433.82 GiB**, ~54 GiB/GPU even shard + replicated dense + KV ≈ 55–60 GiB/GPU < 80, ~20 GiB headroom, COMPUTED) — so **resident EP is feasible by capacity**, the lever past the measured 0.2324 tok/s cpu-offload wall (vs 0.89 tok/s llama.cpp CPU). Honest scope: the primitive is landed but **not yet** wired into the live forward, and a live resident-EP tok/s witness is gated on three rungs — a real cross-DEVICE NCCL/RCCL collective (CUDA still `cudaSetDevice(0)`, `Caps.Collective=false`), EP live-wiring, and a multi-GPU binary on the box (Go absent). The cpu-offload wall stands as the baseline until then.
- [Blackwell-class hardware readiness map (GB200/GB300 NVL72) (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/BLACKWELL-NVL72-READINESS-MAP-2026-06-29.md) — a grounded plan for running fak better on the latest NVIDIA datacenter hardware. Thesis: fak's scale-out architecture is already **NVL72-shaped** — a coherent KV-**pool** plane (`internal/cachemeta/pool.go` `FabricShareable()`), a hardware-aware residency **ladder** (`hardware.go`, HBM→DRAM→NUMA-far→CXL→Disk), a device-**mesh** collective seam (`compute.go` `CollectiveBackend`), and a bit-exact middle-span **evict** that survives paging (`paged_evict.go`, `#33` GO) — but **every one is a stub today** (a cost model, a CPU-ref collective, `cudaSetDevice(0)`, no FP4 enum). A GB300 NVL72 (one coherent **20.7 TB** HBM3e NVLink domain across 72 GPUs + 36 Grace CPUs, **130 TB/s** in-domain NVLink, **NVFP4** at 0.5 B/param, ~900 GB/s Grace coherent memory) is the physical substrate those planes were written against. Maps each Blackwell lever onto the exact seam it lights up (file-anchored, with `[SHIPPED]`/`[SEAM-ONLY]`/`[GAP]` state), names the four contexts where fak's *specific* levers compound (fleet prefix reuse across a coherent domain; demote-not-evict against a 900 GB/s tier; KV disaggregation at 1.8 TB/s NVLink ~18–36× the planned TCP path; the frontier class in-reach as FP4 weights stop dominating HBM and KV governance becomes the scarce resource), resolves the **one load-bearing tension** (NVFP4 vs the bit-exact-evict moat → tier precision per *plane*: FP4 the weights + V + read-K, keep an f32 `Kraw` shadow for governably-evictable spans, the FP4 analogue of the `#1047` Q8-KV choice), and lays a **hardware-ungated-first** rung ladder (R0 FP4+BF16 `Dtype` + cpu-ref oracle, R1 FP4-KV tier the moat survives, R2 model the NVL72 in the cost planes, R3 KV→bytes serializer — all doable now; R5 NVFP4 device GEMM, R6 NCCL device collective, R7 NVLink/RDMA byte mover, R8 the governed mega-pool — Blackwell-gated). Strategic frame: the hardware doesn't make fak a *faster server* (vLLM/SGLang still win tok/s) — it makes fak's **governance** value mandatory, because a 20.7 TB coherent pool shared by a fleet *forces* the cross-tenant-reuse / provable-evict / placement / disaggregation questions only fak answers with attribution. Asserts **no** measured fak number on Blackwell; every figure is a published spec or a labelled MODELED cost-model projection (the `#44` parity-must-be-measured rule still binds).
- [Cache savings in one session: what fak did and didn't (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/SESSION-CACHE-SAVINGS-ABLATION-2026-06-29.md) — ablates a `fak guard` session (`c6723f7c`, 122 turns) three ways — **no cache (~29.65M projected)**, **normal Anthropic cache (~4.15M, −86%)**, **fak guard (~4.15M, identical)** — to make the attribution honest: the `~25.5M input-token-equiv` headline is the **provider's** prompt cache, which fak **prices** (`cache_pricing.go`, OBSERVED not WITNESSED) but cannot move, because passthrough forwards `req.Raw` verbatim and the `cache_control` prefix stays byte-identical. fak's *own* contribution is a sliver of uncached tool-def tokens (tool-floor prune, ~0.1% of headline) and — the real lever — **1,104 bad tool calls stopped before a wasted round-trip** (1,101 repaired, 3 denied); compaction stayed idle. Also answers **"does that mean fak's features aren't on?"** with a HEAD-verified enablement audit — all six token-savers are default-on (`token_defaults.go`-locked) incl. `ctxview`/`elide` (flipped on 06-28/06-26; #927 closed the #555 passthrough gap), but the cache-breakpoint-near-the-end structure leaves fak only a small uncached tail to act on, by design (a referee), not a misconfig. Two-panel [ablation SVG](docs/notes/session-cache-ablation-2026-06-29.svg) + [enablement-status SVG](docs/notes/session-feature-enablement-2026-06-29.svg).
- [The cache-hit vanity metric: a self-fulfilling prophecy toward over-long sessions (2026-07-01)](docs/notes/CACHE-HIT-VANITY-METRIC-SELF-FULFILLING-2026-07-01.md) — the **incentive** layer under the [frozen-trajectory cache cliff](docs/explainers/frozen-trajectory-cache-cliff.md): prompt-cache hit rate rises with trajectory length by construction (`(T-1)/(T+1)`), so reading it as an efficiency signal is a self-fulfilling prophecy toward over-long sessions — the *same* growing prefix that lifts the hit rate also raises the per-turn bill (19× at 200 turns) and drives context rot, and the "saved-token" headline is ~quadratic in length too. Names both sides (long is rewarded; cutting is punished by a visible hit dip yet repays its one cold prefix in a couple of turns), the counter-metrics to steer on instead (verified-progress-per-token via `dos_status`/`dos_commit_audit`, marginal-cost trend via `tools/ctxcost.py marginal`, flat context via the [relay](docs/notes/CONCEPT-PERPETUAL-SESSIONS-2026-07-01.md)), and the many-agent corollary (short fresh agents + [pay-the-prefix-once](visuals/65-pay-the-prefix-once.svg), not the monolith a hit-rate maximizer would prefer). Runnable: `python tools/cache_curve.py inversion` (new `inversion` subcommand + `turn_cost`/`cum_cost`/`saved_headline`/`cut_break_even_turns`, math pinned by `CostInversion` tests).
- [Audit: recent fak logs — effectiveness + context fidelity (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/AUDIT-recent-fak-logs-effectiveness-fidelity-2026-06-28.md) — the **operational** companion to the [harness-cache-coherence design audit](docs/notes/HARNESS-CACHE-COHERENCE-AUDIT-2026-06-28.md), driven by the *recent* logs on the clone (`.fak/`). Answers the two questions directly. **(1) How effective?** From today's `recent-feature-dogfood` log: vCache `7.13×` telemetry readiness (one workload, OBSERVED), callavoid `2.46×` turn-amplification, cache-value `63%` realized reuse — but the load-bearing finding is the **honest ceiling**: `internal/cachevalueledger/ledger.go:111-119` hard-codes the only publishable framing as `~1.0× single-session (vs-naive 1/(1-reuse) excluded per #1066)`, and the `--floor 0.751` gate *correctly abstained* because `MinGateTurns=8` and only 5 turns were present. The dogfood's overall `ACTION` verdict comes from two **honest** failures, neither a context fault: a probe that refuses to call a *planned* 2× a *telemetry-witnessed* 2× (`recent_feature_dogfood.py:348-355` requires `active_source=="telemetry"`), and a transient assembler build error (`internal/model/quant_amd64_kquant.s` `CMPQ $0,AX` — green at HEAD when re-checked, so a stale-log artifact). **(2) How do we know it isn't butchering context?** Five tested invariants — verbatim-prefix byte-splice (`anthropic_compact.go`/`anthropic_elide.go`, a turn only counts `fired` if the protected prefix was byte-identical, else `prefix_mismatch`, `metrics.go:104`), bail-to-identity on any ambiguity with a closed reason vocabulary, bounded+marked+never-total elision loss (head+tail, last-4 working set untouched), the witness-gated quarantine moat (`recall.go:18-22`), and single-source WITNESSED/OBSERVED-separated attribution. Names the three **honest fidelity limits** rather than hiding them: KV prefix-elision is admittedly NOT bit-exact (`exact=false`, "a re-RoPE cannot un-see attended history", `kvmmu_elide_test.go:51-76`); the quarantine-survives-harness-summary hole is *detected* (`QuarantineAtRisk`) not fixed; and the `PreCompact` suppression **actuator is not yet wired** (`harness_coherence.go:283`, #1133 open) — fak today *measures* the harness butchering context but does not yet *prevent* it. Verdict: effectiveness REAL-but-fenced (passes net-true-value), fidelity STRONG-by-construction + HONEST-about-the-gaps. The mechanism even acted **live on the auditing session** (a `ctxview`-elided turn + a credential-shaped string paged out, both announced with a stub). Lists three checkable `not yet` follow-ons.
### The context-safety series (epic #1217)

- [Context-safety doctrine (#1218, C1 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-visuals-tracking-1217.md) — the C1 design note for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): states the **property** (context safety = value-preservation, the **dual** of the security floor — the security floor proves a *bad* call can't get *in*, this floor proves a *good* call's value doesn't silently get *lost* when fak sheds context, and shows it as a cross-checkable picture, not a number you must trust), grounded in why a single number can't carry it (the measured `ctxplan/signalnoise` cache-hit denominator artifact: 0.88→0.99 mechanically with length). Names the **context-event source of truth** — the hash-chained journal `internal/journal/journal.go:59-90` (`CAP_FAULT | CAP_EVICT | CAP_VERSION_BIND` rows, `Seq`/`PrevHash`/`Hash`/`Witness`, replayable via `Verify`/`VerifyRows`) plus the two secondary sources (`engine/capacity_adapter.go` `CacheEventRecorder`; `cachemeta/kvtransfer.go` `KVTransfer` ok/missed/fault). Decomposes "real retained value" into **four guarantees**, each bound to a real cited witness seam: **G1** bit-identity `max\|Δ\|=0` evict-correctness (`kvcache.go:44-105`; `paged_evict_test.go:84`, `longrope_test.go:179`), **G2** saved-vs-realized reuse-bit + correctness (WITNESSED `fak_gateway_kv_prefix_reused_tokens_total` `cachewitness.go:155` reconciled against `prefix_coherence.go:51`), **G3** S/N + Faults paired (`signalnoise.go` `Ratio()`/`FaultRatio()`/`Grade()` `starving`), **G4** cell-integrity (`recall.PageSyndrome` five-axis digest re-derivation). States the **self-checking-visual doctrine** (A paired-axis / B conservation roll-up / C provenance lane / D re-derivation, generalizing the `RestoreIndex(ix.Image()) == ix` rebuild-and-prove-equal pattern to the rendered visual) and the **honesty fences** (value-preservation kept distinct from the security floor AND the #1147 self-tax plane; WITNESSED/OBSERVED/MODELED never summed; an un-witnessed guarantee is a `not yet` not a result; design-only). Names the honest `not yet` gaps — the re-derivation witness (C9 #1227), the cell-integrity visual surface, the DOS refusal tokens (C13 #1231) — rather than rendering them green. Research/design only; sibling of the C2 failure-mode catalog [#1219](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1219).
- [Context-safety failure-mode catalog (#1219, C2 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-failure-mode-catalog-1217.md) — the C2 child of the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): every failure the value-preservation plane must surface, each mapped to its **witness** (the journal `Kind` / typed verdict / metric / test that catches it), the visual **primitive** that renders it, and the **cross-check** (paired-axis / conservation roll-up / provenance lane / re-derivation) that goes red on it. Six grounded rows — GDN evict panic (#1143, `RecurrentEvictUnsupportedError` + `fak_bgloop_up`-vs-decode-rate liveness light), evict-on-recurrent refused (the `refused` conservation segment), saved-but-never-reused (the WITNESSED `fak_gateway_kv_prefix_reused_tokens_total` gap chart, never blended with OBSERVED provider `cache_read`), S/N-up-but-faults-up (the `ctxplan/signalnoise` `Ratio()`+`FaultRatio()` `starving` grade), cell rot (#782 `recall.PageSyndrome`'s five-axis digest/quarantine/durability/witness/trust-epoch re-derivation), tier page-down missed (`cachemeta.KVTransfer` ok/missed/fault, ties #985) — each cited to a real `file:line`. Plus two failures with **no closing witness** kept as honest named gaps (rendered-visual-drift → C9/C10; harness-rewrite-defeats-the-shed → the `internal/compactcohere` follow-on), because the doctrine's rule is that a named gap is catalog content and only a *silent* gap is the invisible-destruction failure the epic exists to kill. Research/design only; sibling of the C1 doctrine note [#1218](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1218).
- [Context-safety saved-vs-realized gap (#1221, C4 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-saved-vs-realized-gap-1217.md) — the C4 value-decomposition spec (MVP slice) for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the one value-gap that matters — **claimed-saved tokens at page-out time vs later-realized reuse** — built from two WITNESSED taps, **D1** (claimed-saved: journal `CAP_EVICT` row + `internal/engine/cacheevents.go:304-317` `CacheEventRecorder`) and **D2** (realized reuse: `internal/cachewitness` `KVPrefixWitness.ReuseRatio()` `cachewitness.go:155` over `fak_gateway_kv_prefix_reused_tokens_total` `metrics.go:1531`, fed by `cacheobs.Default.Snapshot()`). Specs the `SavedRealizedGap` datum (`Gap = ClaimedSaved - RealizedReused`, the error bar; a positive gap is an over-claim fak never realized), the cross-checks it carries (A paired-axis saved-with-realized + D re-derive both from tamper-evident sources, the C9 checker), and the load-bearing **provenance fence**: the gap is **never blended with the OBSERVED provider `cache_read`** — grounded in the measured cache-hit denominator artifact (`cache_read` climbs to ~1.0 mechanically with length, so a gap built on it would close to zero precisely when the window is most bloated). Binds guarantee G2 of the C1 doctrine (#1218); renders failure F3 of the C2 catalog (#1219); reconciled against the C14 coherence witness (#1232); re-derived by C9 (#1227). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety visual-primitive schemas (#1223, C6 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-visual-primitive-schemas-1217.md) — the C6 schema spec (MVP slice) for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the **data schema of each of the six visual primitives** — what each consumes (a real `file:line` witnessed source), its shape (time-series / roll-up / heatmap, **never a single point**), and the cross-check (A/B/C/D) it carries — riding a shared `CtxSafetyPanel` / `PanelPoint` envelope that reuses the `internal/covmatrix:33` `Schema = "fak-coverage-matrix/1"` versioned-payload precedent, the `internal/scoreboard/render.go:41` `FromPayload` text/emoji fallback, and the `internal/cdb/report_html.go:91` `(*Image).HTMLReport` HTML precedent. The six: **(1)** event ribbon (journal `CAP_EVICT`/`CAP_FAULT` + `cacheevents.go` `CacheEventRecorder` + `cachemeta/kvtransfer.go:34-48` `KVTransfer` ok/missed/fault), **(2)** saved-vs-realized gap (C4), **(3)** S/N+Faults strip (`signalnoise.go:69/:81/:249`), **(4)** evict-correctness heatmap (`kvcache.go:44-105` + `recall.PageSyndrome`), **(5)** residency conservation bar (`ctxresidency.go:16-31` `State` + `:39-42` `EvictBlastRadius`), **(6)** liveness light (`fak_bgloop_up` vs decode-rate). The envelope structurally forbids a single trust-me point (every point carries a paired axis, a conservation segment, a provenance lane, and a re-derivability bit). Schemas doctrine A–D of the C1 note (#1218); renders the C2 failures (#1219); rendering *home* is C11 (#1229). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety re-derivation checker (#1227, C9 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-rederivation-checker-1217.md) — the C9 **keystone** spec (MVP slice) for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the contract under which a rendered context-safety visual is **RED unless every number re-derives from a tamper-evident source**. It generalizes the in-tree rebuild-and-prove-equal discipline from `internal/ctxplan/image.go`, the journal `Verify`/`VerifyRows` checks, WITNESSED metrics, git evidence, and recall digests to the rendered visual. A non-re-derivable number emits a structured refusal instead of an unearned OK. CI-gate sibling is C10 (#1228). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety reuse-correctness witness (#1232, C14 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-reuse-correctness-witness-1217.md) — the C14 second-derivation spec for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the reuse-**correctness** witness that the C4 saved-vs-realized reuse **count** must reconcile against, so a high reuse count over a *broken* prefix is caught as value that only looks real. Binds two existing fail-closed `internal/cachemeta` verdicts — `prefix_coherence.go:51` `EvaluatePrefixCoherence(...) PrefixBreakDirective` (uncertainty → break the prefix, never an optimistic "probably fine") + `prefix_stability.go:193` `StabilityReport` (produced by `AnalyzeStability` `:203`) — as the second derivation, and states the reconciliation rule `Reconciled ⟺ (RealizedReused == 0) ∨ (Coherent ∧ Stable)`: a non-zero realized-reuse count is credited only if the reused prefix is coherent AND stable, else the gap panel refuses green with `VALUE_UNRECONCILED` (C13). Honest gap named: the count (`cachewitness`/`cacheobs`) and the coherence/stability verdicts are computed on separate paths and **never compared today** — that comparison is the witness this note specs. Deepens guarantee G2(b) of the C1 doctrine (#1218); reconciliation partner of the C4 gap (#1221). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety DOS refusal tokens (#1231, C13 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-dos-refusal-tokens-1217.md) — the C13 structured-refusal spec for the context-safety epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the three new `dos.toml [reasons]` tokens a panel emits to **refuse green with a verifiable reason** instead of rendering an unearned OK — `SAFETY_UNRECOVERED` (a context-removal event with no tamper-evident journal row), `VALUE_UNRECONCILED` (the reuse count credits value the C14 coherence/stability witness refutes), `SAFETY_UNWITNESSED` (a rendered number with no tamper-evident source). Each gets a category / refusal / summary / fix / see_also and an **oracle condition** an independent check (C9 / C14) decides — verified against the live `dos_refuse_reasons` schema and fak's `L3_PAGE_DIGEST_MISMATCH` verify-rung precedent. Witnessed gap as the anchor: all three return `known=false, UNCLASSIFIED` via `dos_check_reason` today, so a panel cannot emit them until declared; the TOML sketch matches the real `[reasons.*]` grammar (`dos.toml:424/445`). The refusal vocabulary the C9 checker (#1227) + C4 gap (#1221) + C14 reconciliation (#1232) emit. Research/design only.
- [Context-safety bit-identity evict-correctness signal (#1220, C3 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-bit-identity-evict-correctness-1217.md) — the C3 spec for guarantee **G1** of epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the bit-identity (`max|Δ|=0`) evict-correctness signal as a per-EVENT witnessed datum feeding primitive (4), the evict-correctness heatmap. The guarantee ALREADY EXISTS and is ALREADY TESTED — the job is to SURFACE and cross-check it, not invent a new compute claim. Binds the typed boundary `internal/model/kvcache.go` (`RecurrentEvictUnsupportedError` :54 / `CanEvict` :71 / `Evict` :94 / `TryEvict` :105) and the two existing bit-identity tests `paged_evict_test.go:84` `TestPagedEvictBitIdenticalToContiguous` (mid-span `[2,5)` evict on a non-block-aligned page table is byte-for-byte identical to the contiguous Evict — cache AND next-token logits) + `longrope_test.go:179` `TestLongropeEvictEqualsNeverSaw`. Cross-check C + D (the number 0 proves its own correctness; re-derive against the test/verdict). Every citation adversarially verified. Surfaces guarantee G1; renders into the C6 heatmap (#1223). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety S/N + Faults paired series (#1222, C5 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-sn-faults-paired-series-1217.md) — the C5 spec for guarantee **G3** of epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the signal-to-noise + Faults PAIRED time-series (not the current single point read) feeding primitive (3). The point: a shed that RAISES S/N by STARVING the turn (eliding spans then demand-paging them back as faults) is value destruction wearing a cleanup costume, and a single-axis "S/N improved" visual is a lie by construction — so the two orthogonal axes are charted TOGETHER. Binds `internal/ctxplan/signalnoise.go` `Ratio()` :69 (caching/length-invariant signal axis) + `FaultRatio()` :81 (orthogonal under-resident axis) + `Grade()` :249-261 (lean/ok/bloated/**starving**), over `SignalNoise` (token-count fields :44-56, `UnaccountedTokens` kept in the denominator :48-51 so the ratio can't over-claim) computed from `ctxplan.Outcome` (`learn.go:25-29`, span-ID slices). Cross-check A (S/N WITH Faults — raise-S/N-by-starving spikes the visible Faults lane) + B + D; the `starving` grade is the named failure verdict (witnessed by `signalnoise_test.go:89`). Citations adversarially verified. Renders C2 failure F4 (#1219). Research/design only.
- [Context-safety conservation roll-up contract (#1224, C7 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-conservation-rollup-1217.md) — the C7 spec for doctrine mechanism **B** of epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the conservation roll-up feeding primitive (5), the residency conservation bar. The contract: a roll-up whose segments are WITNESSED sub-counts that MUST sum to the total — resident = signal + noise + unaccounted; evicted = bit-clean + reset + refused — so if the segments don't sum the visual is VISIBLY broken (the eye does the check, no second tool). Binds `internal/ctxplan/signalnoise.go` (`UnaccountedTokens` in the denominator :48-51, `ResidentTokens` as the sum :57-59 so it can't over-claim) + `internal/ctxresidency/ctxresidency.go` (`State` :16-31 resident/evictable/held, `BlastRadius{Tokens,DependentEntries}` :39-42, `Snapshot` roll-up :73-89). The `refused` evicted-segment ties to C2 failure F2 + `serve --reset-on-budget` (`cmd/fak/serve.go:110`). Specs the `ConservationRollup` datum + the RED-on-mismatch (sum≠total) rule. Citations adversarially verified (no fixes needed). Doctrine B; primitive 5. Research/design only.
- [Context-safety CI gate (#1228, C10 of epic #1217) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/context-safety-ci-gate-1217.md) — the C10 spec for epic [#1217](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/1217): the build-time enforcement that reds `make ci` when a shipped visual's numbers stop re-deriving — turning C9's per-visual `RederivationVerdict` into a green gate. The load-bearing fence (#1228's core ask): **do not red on noise** — a single transient scrape skew is one tick and is never the build verdict; only a PERSISTENT mismatch reds, via a consecutive-streak / change-point rule lifted from `internal/loopmgr/restart.go` (`MaxAttempts` + `ResetAfter` clear window) and `internal/rsiloop/metarsi.go` (`Window:20, MinEscalations:2`).
