- [DOS fresh-install value dogfood, from the laptop (2026-06-25)](docs/notes/DOS-FRESH-INSTALL-VALUE-DOGFOOD-LAPTOP-2026-06-25.md) — the outward fresh-user counterpart to the inward dogfood loop (`internal/dogfoodscore`): a genuine `pip install dos-kernel` into a clean venv on the laptop, then the first-60-seconds value path captured verbatim. One dependency (PyYAML only), ~20s install, and `dos quickstart` (1.4s) lands the whole pitch — an agent claims it shipped AUTH1+AUTH2, git says only AUTH1 landed (exit 0 vs exit 1), then a lease-lane collision is refused not silently overwritten. `dos commit-audit` caught a planted lie (subject claims a code fix, diff touches only a README → UNWITNESSED, exit 1) and passed an honest doc commit. Records the honest newcomer friction too: `dos --version` is not a flag, `--hooks auto` needs a real agent repo, and the install timing was warm-cache.

- [idea-scout triage: MCPPrivacyDetector — protocol-induced MCP-server leakage; prior art + a threat fak already gates at runtime (result-admit), static analyzer not adopted (2026-06-23)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-privacy-leakage-triage-2026-06-23.md)
- [idea-scout triage: Tool-Guard / isolated planning — a threat axis fak does not yet gate (2026-06-23)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-tool-guard-isolated-planning-triage-2026-06-23.md)
- [idea-scout triage: relinking at the compression boundary — fak immune (extractive compression + call-not-prompt floor); prior art + a guarded invariant for future generative summarizers (2026-06-23)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-relinking-compression-boundary-triage-2026-06-23.md)
- [idea-scout triage: CLAWAUDIT / source-code audit of the agent runtime layer — prior art validating 'the runtime IS the boundary' thesis + an audit methodology to mirror on fak's own seven components; static-rule tool not adopted (2026-06-24)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-agent-runtime-source-audit-triage-2026-06-24.md)
- [idea-scout triage: PrologMCP — a stateful Prolog solver exposed over MCP; a capability paper (symbolic delegation), not the MCP-security topic it surfaced under; close / not adopted — a server fak GOVERNS (sits behind the gateway), not a kernel component; its three named design properties each map to a shipped fak server-facing seam (2026-06-24)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-prologmcp-symbolic-tool-triage-2026-06-24.md)
- [idea-scout triage: agentic surveillance / SurveilBench — a trust topology INVERTED from fak's (the agent surveils its own user for a provider); fak gates the egress 'send-the-report' step ONLY when the operator runs the gateway for the user, and the paper's evasion defense (prompt-injection repurposed) is the inverse of fak's injection-is-non-load-bearing floor; prior art to cite + a named scope boundary, mechanism not adopted (2026-06-25)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-agentic-surveillance-evasion-triage-2026-06-25.md)
- [idea-scout triage: AgenticOS / intent-oriented secure OS (#770) (2026-06-26)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-agenticos-intent-os-triage-2026-06-26.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.21129 (Zhao et al., "AgenticOS: An Intent-Oriented Secure Operating System Architecture for Autonomous AI Agents"): "resource exposure + permission checks" OS security fails once an agent runtime is compromised, because the attacker composes POSIX primitives past the user's task authorization; AgenticOS reframes the OS from a "resource manager" into an "intent filter" — agents submit structured **intent declarations** → the system synthesizes a least-privilege env with **mandatory mediation, auditing, and information-flow constraints** — via a four-layer kernel (Ghost Kernel, Logic Shutter, Agent Capsule, Semantic Boundary Gateway) + Intent ABI / Manifest-Only Runtime / Weaver capability generation / native-Skill admission. Verdict: **prior art to cite** — the cleanest **OS-altitude** statement of fak's founding premise, and its four required properties map onto shipped fak seams that realize them **in userspace** (default-deny capability floor, the one in-process syscall boundary, the witness/decision journal, result-admit quarantine + `wirescreen` + `SinkGate`). Same side of the trust boundary as fak (not a threat). The four-layer **OS-rebuild** architecture + Weaver synthesis are **not adopted** — fak's bet is one userspace binary at the tool-call seam ([one-binary-one-surface](docs/explainers/one-binary-one-surface.md)), not a new kernel; the honest fence is that an OS-altitude filter mediates a strictly larger primitive set at a strictly larger adoption cost.
- [idea-scout triage: Kamera / position-invariant multimodal KV cache (#583) (2026-06-26)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-kamera-position-invariant-kv-triage-2026-06-26.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.23581 (Ma, Eitzinger, Koestler, Wellein, "Kamera: Unified Position-Invariant Multimodal KV Cache for Training-Free Reuse"): prefix caches serve reuse only at a fixed leading position, so multimodal agents re-encode the same video frames / UI screenshots on every look-back; naive non-prefix KV reuse loses the **cross-chunk conditioning** a chunk absorbs from neighbours, and the loss is **asymmetric** — the direct readout is recovered free by the standard **state-merge**, but a diffuse low-rank residue in deep layers remains, invisible to single-hop retrieval yet **halving multi-hop accuracy**; Kamera repairs it with a training-free **low-rank conditioning patch** + one **RoPE re-rotation** operator across MLA/GQA/MHA (recovers full task accuracy on MM-NIAH + 2-page doc-QA, reconstructs KV within bf16 rounding in a production SGLang kernel across six backbones; strongest in vision/video). Verdict: **prior art to cite** — the paper fak's own [ON-DEMAND-CONTEXT-KV-REUSE](docs/notes/ON-DEMAND-CONTEXT-KV-REUSE-2026-06-19.md) note §2.2/§2.3 anticipated and left open (CacheBlend cross-attention loss + MiniPIC position-independent-but-insufficient), now characterized precisely and given a training-free repair — **and a candidate repair** for the conservative RECOMPUTE/REFUSE fault fak's #109 non-prefix-KV audit (`internal/model/nonprefix_kvreuse_audit_test.go`) takes by design. **Adopt only at fak's reserved corrective-path tier (ON-DEMAND §6 Step 5, at #109 × the governed vision seam #399) — NOT in this increment** (a GPU/kernel epic + it needs a gate fak lacks). **One named invariant:** Kamera's bar is full task accuracy + within-bf16-rounding KV, not fak's bit-exact last-logit; because the loss is asymmetric a patched reuse can pass fak's single-last-logit #109 oracle (a single-hop-shaped probe) while halving multi-hop accuracy — so any future patch that promotes a RECOMPUTE fault to a served HIT must clear a **multi-hop** cross-chunk-binding quality probe, never the single-logit check alone. Not a threat (a performance capability on the same side).
- [idea-scout triage: joint intent+harm verification (#910) (2026-06-26)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-intent-harm-joint-verification-triage-2026-06-26.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26377 (Thota, Lei, Thangaraj, Jonnalagadda, Nilizadeh, "Verifying Intent and Harm: A Unified Defense Against LLM-Generated Threats"): prompt-only and response-only defenses miss attacks that exploit the **separation** between adversarial **intent** (in the prompt) and actionable **harm** (only in the response); the paper proposes a verify-before-deliver framework — a specialized **Intent Analyst** (scores prompt intent) + a specialized **Harm Analyst** (scores response harm) + a **Judge** that resolves conflicts (reported F1 0.90→0.95, ASR 4.1%, benign-sensitive FPR 0.12→0.06 across jailbreaks/prompt-injection/phishing/cyber-abuse/harmful-content, tested under adaptive attackers who know the verifier). Verdict: **prior art to cite** — the join-intent-with-harm thesis is fak's **two-sided adjudication** design at the detector altitude: the kernel already splits the same axis structurally (`k.Decide` folds **only** the pre-call **intent** chain; `k.AdmitResult` is the in-code documented **dual of Decide** and folds the result-side **harm** chain — quarantine + IFC taint + `wirescreen`/`SinkGate` egress floor), and the `dos verify` / `dos commit-audit` referee re-checks claimed intent against the actual diff-borne harm. The three-LLM-analyst + Judge **mechanism is NOT adopted** — it is a learned detector, and fak's recorded position is **detector-is-not-the-floor** ([defensive-misdirection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-defensive-misdirection-triage-2026-06-23.md), [AUC-is-not-detection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-probe-auc-evaluation-protocol-triage-2026-06-23.md)); the paper's own adaptive-attacker result is the evidence that a learned verifier is evadable where fak's default-deny capability floor the model "can't talk past" is not. **Named residual:** the Harm Analyst scores **content harm in text** (the model emitting phishing/harmful words) that fak's capability gate does not score at the tool-call seam — a genuine best-effort-detector rung fak hosts **above** its floor, not on the load-bearing path. Not a threat (a parallel defense on the same side of the boundary).
- [idea-scout triage: ShareLock / multi-tool threshold tool-poisoning against MCP (#911) (2026-06-26)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-sharelock-multitool-threshold-poisoning-triage-2026-06-26.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.27027 (Liu, Han, Liu, Dong, Ruan, "ShareLock: A Stealthy Multi-Tool Threshold Poisoning Attack Against MCP"): a Tool Poisoning Attack that fragments its malicious instruction with **Shamir's (t, n) threshold secret-sharing** across MULTIPLE innocent-looking MCP tool descriptions, reconstructing the hidden instruction only on a **trigger (a server update)** — claiming **information-theoretic** stealth (no description below threshold reveals intent), >90% success, and provable outperformance of single-tool poisoning against **tool-description-based detectors**. Verdict: **prior art to cite + a real threat whose consequence the capability floor already contains** — ShareLock attacks the **detection layer** fak deliberately keeps **off the load-bearing path** (`CLAIMS.md` "Honest ceiling": detection is ~100% evadable, **non-load-bearing**), so its core move (disperse-to-evade a description scanner) is **neutralized by construction** against a floor that never reads the descriptions for a verdict and gates the resulting **action** (default-deny capability lock, units 15/29) and **result** (result-admit quarantine, unit 48) instead. ShareLock is the cleanest external **information-theoretic** proof of fak's *detector-is-not-the-floor* thesis, alongside [defensive-misdirection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-defensive-misdirection-triage-2026-06-23.md) / [AUC-is-not-detection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-probe-auc-evaluation-protocol-triage-2026-06-23.md) / [MCPPrivacyDetector](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-privacy-leakage-triage-2026-06-23.md). **Named residual:** fak does not screen inbound third-party tool descriptions, and a poisoned description can still induce a **policy-ALLOWED** call with malicious arguments — the standing residual of any capability floor, **contained** (not intercepted) by IFC taint + egress sink-gating. The Shamir-sharing mechanism is **not adopted** (adding a description scanner is the anti-pattern ShareLock refutes); the smallest honest follow-on is to treat a downstream **tool-definition change** as a witnessed provenance event (the trigger fires on server update), filed against the `fak_refute_witness` / `fak_changes` seam. No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` — surfaced and scored correctly (topic mcp-security, score 53).
- [idea-scout triage: teaching software engineering with LLM + MCP (#1009) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-llm-mcp-se-education-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.19167 (Chen, Keung, Li, Shao, Li, Ma, "Teaching Software Engineering with LLM and MCP Integration: From Classroom to Industry Practice"): a software-engineering **EDUCATION / pedagogy** study that embeds LLM- and MCP-driven tools into a collaborative **teaching model** (daily teaching, code assistance, engineering simulations) plus industry-internship partnerships, to bridge classroom instruction and industrial workflows. Verdict: **close / not adopted — out of kernel scope** — fak is a runtime trust + performance gate at the tool-call seam, not a curriculum, teaching platform, or learning framework; the paper proposes **no system fak would adopt, no attack to defend against, and no mechanism-level prior art** for fak's adjudication / cross-turn-reuse design (a clean off-mission sibling of the [PrologMCP capability/security miscategorization](docs/notes/RESEARCH-prologmcp-symbolic-tool-triage-2026-06-24.md)). **Recorded residual (context, not a citation):** the paper is one more external data point that **MCP is moving into mainstream industrial — and now academic — SE practice**, the adoption trend that makes a default-deny capability floor on MCP tool calls worth shipping; it is kept as *why-now* context, never cited as prior art for a fak mechanism. **Scout calibration:** surfaced under `mcp-security` (score 47, the lowest of the day's three hits) on a bare `model context protocol` / `mcp (title)` keyword match + freshness — a true **on-topic-by-keyword, off-mission-by-content** candidate, the scout working **as designed** (judges new-and-on-topic, never worth-building; hands to human triage). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py`.
- [idea-scout triage: where do CoT training gains land in agents? (#1008) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-cot-training-gains-agents-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26935 (Liu, Wang, Jing, Zhou, Liu, "Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?"): an **interpretability / training-analysis** study that asks where CoT-training gains land — in **changing the action through generated reasoning** (CoT actions) or in **predicting the action directly from the prompt** with no CoT (prompt actions) — and finds, across checkpoints, that **prompt-action quality improves substantially**, i.e. much of the gain lands in the direct prompt→action mapping rather than in reasoning-driven action change, consistent with prior **CoT-unfaithfulness / post-hoc-rationalization** findings. Verdict: **prior art to cite — not adopted, not a threat** — fak never reads the model's verbalized reasoning for a verdict and never trusts a self-report; it gates the resulting **action** at the tool-call seam (default-deny capability floor) and re-checks claimed work against the **diff** (`dos verify` / `dos commit-audit`), so this is independent **training-time** evidence for fak's **detector-is-not-the-floor / witness-not-self-report** bet (the action is largely fixed by the prompt before the CoT is written, so a CoT-reading detector audits a post-hoc narrative). It is the **CoT-as-explanation** sibling of the contested **attention-as-explanation** demotion in fak's reward-over-spans epic [#861](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/861)/[#862](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/862) ([witnessed-span-attention-validity](docs/notes/RESEARCH-witnessed-span-attention-validity-2026-06-26.md)): a self-generated explanation is a **proposal to verify against an action-level witness**, never an accepted causal account. **Recorded residual (NOT adopted):** high prompt-action quality means CoT tokens are non-load-bearing on the easy mass of steps — a **token-economy** lever for the `fak dojo` gym ([#951](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/951)) / token-saving-defaults program — but only behind a **per-model, per-task accuracy acceptance gate fak lacks**, and the paper itself shows CoT still **changes the action on the hard tail**, so blind CoT-dropping is unsafe and explicitly not shipped. **Scout calibration:** surfaced under `agent-model-arch` (score 50) on genuine `training (title)` / `checkpoint` / `reasoning` terms + freshness — correctly on-topic and **closer-to-mission** than the day's #1009 pedagogy hit; the scout working **as designed**. No change to `tools/idea_scout.py`.
- [idea-scout triage: MIRROR / novelty-constrained memory-guided MCTS red-teaming for agentic RAG (#1007) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mirror-novelty-mcts-redteam-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26793 (Singh, Murillo, Sekiya, Unno, Suga, "MIRROR: Novelty-Constrained Memory-Guided MCTS Red-Teaming for Agentic RAG"): an **offensive cross-surface attack GENERATOR** for multimodal agentic RAG — memory-guided Monte Carlo tree search that conditions candidate generation on retrieved context under a **deterministic Novelty Gate** (rejects any candidate matching the retrieval set under normalized comparison) across **text poisoning / image injection / direct-query / orchestrator-level tool manipulation**, motivated by measuring **73-84% exact template duplication** in existing surface-specific red-teaming. Verdict (three-part, the shape of [#909](docs/notes/RESEARCH-out-of-band-injection-defense-taxonomy-triage-2026-06-26.md) / [#911](docs/notes/RESEARCH-sharelock-multitool-threshold-poisoning-triage-2026-06-26.md)): **(1) prior art to cite** — MIRROR is the **SOTA academic form of fak's own dynamic red-team** (`internal/agentdojo`'s `{plain,obfuscated,paraphrased}` adaptivity axis + `examples/agentdojo-redteam`'s marker-free generative expander) and the **named answer** to the #909 follow-on (make fak's AgentDojo-style ASR battery adaptive, defense-aware, and **cross-surface**, not a static injection set); its deterministic Novelty Gate is the formal sibling of fak's own `tools/idea_scout.py` dedup/novelty rungs (seen-cache + Jaccard near-dup rejection). **(2) A real threat whose CONSEQUENCE the structural floor already contains** — MIRROR's entire edge is **novelty engineered to evade template/pattern detectors**, but fak's full-stack defense taints by **PROVENANCE not content**, so the egress/destructive sink is barred *"regardless of phrasing"* (`internal/agentdojo/agentdojo.go`: ASR(full-stack)==0 vs ASR(detection-only)>0) — the novelty axis is **invariant against the floor by construction**, the same outcome as [ShareLock #911](docs/notes/RESEARCH-sharelock-multitool-threshold-poisoning-triage-2026-06-26.md); the novelty advantage lands entirely on the detection-only layer fak keeps **off the load-bearing path** (~100% evadable, by design). **Named residual:** containment is not a *measured number* — fak has no adaptive cross-surface ASR-under-containment metric, and the **image-injection** (#290, `internal/model/multimodal`, fail-closed `MultimodalModeQuarantine`) / **orchestrator** (gateway/MCP, adjudicated tool call) surfaces are governed but unmeasured against an adaptive generator. **(3) Mechanism NOT adopted** — fak is a defense; the one-binary zero-dep kernel does not generate attacks (no MCTS / memory / LLM-conditioned generator on the request path; `OUT_OF_DIRECTION` would refuse it). The smallest honest follow-on is the **evaluation upgrade** (expand `internal/agentdojo` toward a novelty-constrained cross-surface generator + add the image/orchestrator surfaces, turning the standing `docs/industry-scorecard/security.md` "no measured adaptive ASR" fence into a tracked agentdojo-lane task), filed not built — with the fence that MIRROR is cited as a **methodology + a contained threat**, never as evidence fak's containment is adaptively robust. No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` — surfaced and scored correctly (topic `prompt-injection-defense`, score 50).
- [idea-scout triage: how reliable is your jailbreak judge? / ASR-judge calibration + adversarial robustness (#1097) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-jailbreak-judge-asr-reliability-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.25487 (Yang Gao, "How Reliable Is Your Jailbreak Judge? Calibration and Adversarial Robustness of Automated ASR Scoring"): a **measurement-validity** study that checks the automated **judge** assigning nearly every reported attack-success-rate (ASR) — a safety classifier or a prompted chat model — against **596 human-labeled** HarmBench completions, then attacks it. The two families fail in opposite ways: the dedicated classifier **over-flags** (P **0.835**, R **0.974**); three LLM-as-judges keep high precision (**0.81-0.94**) but **erratic recall (0.06-0.65)**, so the same responses yield very different ASR by judge. **Benign framing wrappers** that leave the harmful text intact flip **every LLM-judge 57-100%** (a single prepended refusal sentence = **39-88%**); the classifier resists surface attacks (**≤6.7%**) but a small-budget **white-box GCG** on its open weights flips **70%** of its confident true positives (21/30) — an 80-sample audit confirms the flips left the harm intact. Verdict: **prior art to cite — not adopted, not a threat** — it is measured, external evidence for fak's **detector-is-not-the-floor / witness-not-self-report** thesis and a direct validation of fak's **choice of success criterion**: fak's own ASR (`asr_fullstack=0` = 0/38, `asr_detection=0.763` = 29/38, `benign_completion_rate=1`) is assigned by **no judge model at all** — `internal/agentdojo/agentdojo.go` computes `succeeded := injectionReachedContext && sinkExecuted` from deterministic adjudicator verdicts (`VerdictQuarantine` / `VerdictDeny`; the code comment reads *"Deterministic, in-process, no model"*), `catch_reasons` drawn from the closed structural vocabulary (`TRUST_VIOLATION` 29 / `MALFORMED` 9). Because there is no judge model in fak's success decision, the three attack families the paper documents (framing wrappers, prepended-refusal, GCG-on-judge-weights) are **out of fak's metric by construction** — you cannot frame your way past a `VerdictDeny` that gates **PROVENANCE** (tainted→sink), not content (same shape as [MIRROR #1007](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mirror-novelty-mcts-redteam-triage-2026-06-27.md) / [ShareLock #911](docs/notes/RESEARCH-sharelock-multitool-threshold-poisoning-triage-2026-06-26.md)). The metric-side twin of [AUC-is-not-detection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-probe-auc-evaluation-protocol-triage-2026-06-23.md) / [defensive-misdirection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-defensive-misdirection-triage-2026-06-23.md) and the [FRONTIER survey](docs/notes/FRONTIER-AGENT-LEADERBOARD-SURVEY-2026-06.md) HAL *"benchmarks gameable to near-perfect without solving tasks"* anchor. **Honest fence (carried forward):** the immunity is specific to fak's **structural flow-reachability witness** — the day fak reports a **judge-mediated** ASR (a content-harm probe, or any external benchmark whose ASR is LLM-graded and that fak compares against), it must adopt the paper's protocol: report **judge precision/recall on a human-labeled slice**, report **ASR corrected for judge precision**, and include an **adversarial check of the judge**. **Mechanism not adopted** (a measurement-validity result, no kernel surface). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` — surfaced and scored correctly (topic `prompt-injection-defense`, score 47).
- [idea-scout triage: data-centric survey of privacy in LLM agents (#1098) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-agent-privacy-data-centric-survey-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26627 (Lahjouji, Colaco, "Agents That Know Too Much: A Data-Centric Survey of Privacy in LLM Agents"): a **survey** that organizes LLM-agent privacy around the **data an agent touches** — taxonomizing the data sources (databases, document collections, external APIs, agent memory, cross-session state), the privacy risk each creates (**query leakage, intermediate-result exposure, memory-write vulnerabilities, inter-agent messaging**), and the **governance mechanisms** that address them (it names **information-flow control** + access control), then maps the benchmarks and identifies what is missing. Verdict: **prior art to cite + a real multi-channel threat fak's own IFC governance gates UNEVENLY by provenance** — fak ships one of the two governance families the survey names (`internal/ifc`: a provenance taint `Ledger` (`SourceTaint`) → a `SinkClass` (Egress/Exec/Destructive) → `Policy.Gates` refusing a tainted→gated-sink flow, `DefaultGatedSinks`=EGRESS+DESTRUCTIVE, CaMeL-style `Authorize` escape), so the **final-answer/egress** sink and the **secret-shaped intermediate-result** channel (the [#552 result-admit gate](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-privacy-leakage-triage-2026-06-23.md)) are **gated-by-provenance**; but the survey's **query-issuance** channel is gated-by-capability not by a privacy assessment of the query content, **memory-write** rides the [memory-view contract](docs/notes/MEMORY-VIEW-CONTRACT-2026-06-26.md) read-side only, and **inter-agent messages** cross a boundary the session-scoped taint ledger does not provably propagate across — all **gated-by-capability-only or unmeasured** — and fak reports **no measured privacy-leakage number** (the same *containment-is-not-a-measured-number* residual as [MIRROR #1007](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mirror-novelty-mcts-redteam-triage-2026-06-27.md), now stated for the privacy axis). **Mechanism NOT adopted** (a survey, no kernel component); the smallest honest follow-on is a **per-channel privacy-posture audit** (the survey's data-source x risk grid → each channel labeled gated-by-provenance / gated-by-capability-only / unmeasured) + a **candidate privacy-leakage metric**, FILED not built — with the fence the survey is cited as field framing + a coverage map, never as evidence fak's privacy containment is complete or measured. The field-level umbrella over the source-specific [MCPPrivacyDetector triage (#552)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-privacy-leakage-triage-2026-06-23.md), which instantiates one cell (MCP-server returns). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` — surfaced and scored correctly (topic `prompt-injection-defense`, score 47).
- [idea-scout triage: PRCR / position-rebinding cache reuse for replay-free visual revisiting (#1099) (2026-06-27)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-position-rebinding-cache-reuse-triage-2026-06-27.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26631 (Wang, Ji, Zuo, Ye, Tu, "Position Rebinding Cache Reuse: Replay-Free Visual Revisiting for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning"): interleaved multimodal reasoning revisits visual evidence during multi-step generation and pays for it with **token replay** (re-forwarding selected visual tokens); the shortcut — reusing the historical visual KV cache directly — has a critical failure mode, **cached visual keys are bound to their ORIGINAL positional context**, so reuse under a later decoding context distorts attention and can trigger **SEVERE AUTOREGRESSIVE DECODING COLLAPSE**; PRCR's fix is **position rebinding** (store raw visual KV with original coords, reassign position-compatible coords to selected entries, rebind keys before injecting into the active decoder cache — preserving textual positional continuity + relative visual structure), reporting replay-level-or-better quality, **+5%** average accuracy, and up to **tens-of-thousands×** less visual-revisiting compute. Verdict: **prior art to cite — the collapse-naming sibling of [Kamera #583](docs/notes/RESEARCH-kamera-position-invariant-kv-triage-2026-06-26.md)** and the second hit in the non-prefix / position-aware visual-KV-reuse neighbourhood fak's own [ON-DEMAND-CONTEXT-KV-REUSE](docs/notes/ON-DEMAND-CONTEXT-KV-REUSE-2026-06-19.md) note §2.2/§2.3 anticipated and its **#109** non-prefix-KV audit (`internal/model/nonprefix_kvreuse_audit_test.go`) proves locally. Where Kamera characterized the loss as a diffuse residue that **halves multi-hop accuracy**, PRCR names the **worst case** (an outright collapsed decode) — the strongest external justification yet for fak's **#109 RECOMPUTE/REFUSE** conservative default (*"a failed exactness budget must produce RECOMPUTE/REFUSE, never HIT"*). The sharp insight: fak's shipped EXACT reuse is **prefix-only**, where cached tokens occupy the **same** leading positions on reuse, so PRCR's failure mode **cannot arise on fak's exact surface by construction** — it appears only at the non-prefix splice the #109 audit refuses; PRCR is independent evidence fak's **prefix-only-exact HIT + non-prefix-faults-to-recompute** structure is the safe one. **Adopt the rebinding operator only at fak's reserved corrective-path tier** (ON-DEMAND §6 Step 5, at #109 × the governed vision seam `internal/model/multimodal.go` #399, fail-closed `MultimodalModeQuarantine`) — **NOT in this increment** (a GPU/kernel epic + it needs a quality gate fak lacks). **One named invariant** (carried from the Kamera triage and tightened): a rebinding patch that promotes a RECOMPUTE fault to a served HIT must clear a **multi-hop** cross-chunk quality probe AND a **no-collapse** check — fak's bit-exact-last-logit-or-fault gate already enforces the no-collapse half for free (a within-rounding rebind keeps faulting), so the binding precondition for USING a rebind is the multi-hop probe fak still lacks. Not a threat (a performance capability on the same side). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` — surfaced and scored correctly (topic `kv-prefix-cache-reuse`, score 47).
- [idea-scout triage: progress advantage — a free RL-post-training step reward for LLM agents (#1122) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-progress-advantage-rl-posttraining-triage-2026-06-28.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26080 (Oh, Li, Park, Yeh, Mallick, Li, "Neglected Free Lunch from Post-training: Progress Advantage for LLM Agents"): process reward models give step-level LLM evaluation but are prohibitively hard to build for **agentic** settings (long-horizon, irreversible actions, stochastic feedback defeat annotation + Monte-Carlo at scale); the paper shows **RL post-training already contains the step-level signal** — under a general stochastic MDP the **log-prob ratio between the RL-trained policy and its reference policy exactly recovers the optimal advantage** (termed **progress advantage**: annotation-free, domain-agnostic, a free byproduct of the standard post-training pipeline), validated on test-time scaling / uncertainty quantification / failure attribution across 5 benchmarks + 4 model families, beating confidence baselines and **surpassing dedicated trained reward models**. Verdict: **prior art to cite — at the thesis level, with a sharp boundary** — the paper's "the existing pipeline already contains the step-level signal, so train no dedicated judge" is the **reward-modeling-side sibling** of fak's witness referee (`dos verify` / `dos commit-audit` grade a claim against the **diff git actually recorded** — a derived, annotation-free witness — not a trained reward model or the worker's self-report), and the empirical "free signal beats trained PRMs" is independent support for that **don't-build-the-judge** bet. **Sharp fence:** progress advantage is the policy's **own log-probability** (benchmarked against **confidence-based** baselines), a **model-internal self-report**; fak's recorded **detector-is-not-the-floor / witness-not-self-report** stance ([defensive-misdirection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-defensive-misdirection-triage-2026-06-23.md), [AUC-is-not-detection](docs/notes/RESEARCH-probe-auc-evaluation-protocol-triage-2026-06-23.md)) keeps a signal read off the model's own probabilities **above** the trust floor, never **on** it — the **policy-log-prob sibling** of the CoT-as-explanation demotion in the [#1008 CoT-training triage](docs/notes/RESEARCH-cot-training-gains-agents-triage-2026-06-27.md) and the attention-as-explanation demotion in [#861](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/861)/[#862](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/862) (a self-generated signal is a **proposal verified against an action-level witness**, never an accepted causal account). **Not adopted as a capability** — fak does not RL-post-train, and the "free lunch" needs paired RL-policy + reference-policy log-probs for the SAME served model that **fak's third-party/local fleet has no reference policy for**, so the signal is **not even computable** on fak's models; no kernel surface (same off-mission shape as #1008 / [#1098 privacy survey](docs/notes/RESEARCH-agent-privacy-data-centric-survey-triage-2026-06-27.md)). **Not a threat.** **One recorded residual, NOT adopted:** progress advantage as a **cheap proposal signal** for fak's trajectory graders (the paper's **failure-attribution** application maps onto fak's failure-to-witness need — fleet closure-honesty + the `fak dojo` predict→run→measure→eval→calibrate gym [#951](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/951)), gated behind a paired-policy availability fak lacks and fenced as **never-the-floor**. **Scout calibration:** surfaced under `agent-model-arch` (score 44) on genuine `training (title)` + freshness — correctly on-topic; the **third** recent training-time `agent-model-arch` hit fak cannot adopt as a capability (after #1008; cf. #861/#862), an on-topic-by-keyword/off-mission-by-content pattern worth watching but **not** a scoring bug (these still yield citable thesis-level prior art). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py`.
- [idea-scout triage: Tool Suppression under joint tool-call + JSON-schema constraints (#1121) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-tool-suppression-constraint-tax-triage-2026-06-28.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.25605 (Li, Zhang, Lv, "Constraint Tax in Open-Weight LLMs: An Empirical Study of Tool Calling Suppression Under Structured Output Constraints"): a **serving-side** result — when Tool Calling and a JSON-Schema output constraint are enabled **jointly**, multiple **open-weight** model families **stop invoking tools** while keeping **high schema compliance**, a phenomenon it names **Tool Suppression** (a **"Constraint Tax"** the structured-output constraint silently levies on the tool-calling capability), reproduced across model families/settings; each capability is functional **in isolation**. Verdict: **prior art to cite + empirical validation of fak's [#907](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/907) "one canonical internal shape" decision** — fak models tool-CALLING **as** the schema (`grammar.Grammar` → a `oneOf`-of-tools JSON Schema → the `response_format` carrier OR a native logit mask [#929]), so the **two-competing-constraints** configuration that produces Tool Suppression (an independent output `response_format` stacked on a tool-calling turn) is exactly the one fak's unified shape **avoids**; the paper strengthens #907/#929 rather than asking fak to build anything. **A self-inflicted failure mode fak can induce two ways:** the native guided-decode mask (`internal/model/constraint.go`, behind `FAK_NATIVE_GUIDED_DECODE=1`, which **defaults OFF** — a schema mask is never stacked on a tool-calling turn unless an operator opts in, an accidental protection), and the [#907 passthrough](docs/notes/RESEARCH-structured-output-decoding-2026-06-26.md) (`internal/gateway/http.go:464`) forwarding a client `response_format` to an upstream vLLM/SGLang alongside tools, where any suppression is the **upstream's** behavior — **OBSERVED/relayed, not a fak action** (the controlled-vs-observed conflation boundary). **The load-bearing trust-floor point (why it is labelled `trust-floor`):** a suppressed tool call is **invisible** to fak's default-deny adjudication floor — `internal/adjudicator/decide.go` `Adjudicate(ctx, *abi.ToolCall)` only ever receives a call that **was** emitted, so Tool Suppression is a capability **false-negative below the floor** (no call = nothing to deny = trivially "safe" to a deny-bad-calls gate) that an adjudicator **structurally cannot** catch by adjudicating calls; detection requires **observing the absence** (tool offered + joint schema active + zero calls + schema-valid prose), an **observability** ask fak does not measure today. **Not adopted** as a new mechanism (the right design ships as #907); **not** a classic threat (a constraint-interaction defect, no adversary). **One residual, FILED not built:** a gateway "tool-suppression-suspected" observability counter, gated behind a **served-open-weight reproduction this win32 dev box cannot run** (the "expected a tool call" half needs calibration — shipping it now would be an unwitnessed "expected" claim). No capability code change; no change to `tools/idea_scout.py` (surfaced + scored correctly under topic `tool-call-adjudication`, score 44, on-topic and close-to-mission).
- [idea-scout triage: EpiKV — KV eviction by the epiphany score, no attention matrix (#1123) (2026-06-28)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-epikv-attention-free-eviction-triage-2026-06-28.md) — triage of arXiv:2606.26472 (Kolawole, Smith, "Epiphany-Aware KV Cache Eviction Without the Attention Matrix" / EpiKV): a **serving-side** eviction **policy** — existing methods rank tokens by **attention weight**, which the paper argues is a noisy importance proxy in long reasoning traces AND **prohibits fused kernels** by forcing the model to materialize the attention matrix; EpiKV instead scores tokens by the **epiphany score** (the change in the model's internal representation, read from the forward pass with no attention matrix), training-free and custom-kernel-free. Verdict: **prior art to cite + validation of fak's attention-as-explanation demotion ([#861](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/861)/[#862](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/862)) in the EVICTION-POLICY domain + a candidate fused-kernel-friendly importance POLICY for fak's policy-agnostic exact-eviction MECHANISM**. fak's value-add is the bit-exact mid-run evictor (`internal/model/kvcache.go` `Evict`, the paged twin `internal/model/paged_evict.go` `PagedKV.Evict` [#33], the `internal/kvmmu/` governance evictor) — it evicts a span handed to it and leaves the cache byte-for-byte `== never-saw-it` (`max|Δ|=0`), so the importance scorer is a **swappable input**; fak already shipped ONE policy — [#856](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/856) "attention-informed exact eviction — evict coldest-by-attention via the existing bit-exact evictor" (CLOSED, kvmmu lane), fed by the post-softmax attention mass `internal/model/attn_observer.go` emits. **The load-bearing fak-specific point:** #856's attention mass is materialized for free on fak's CPU softmax seam, but a **fused GPU attention kernel** (FlashAttention/FlashInfer — fak's production decode direction) does NOT materialize the attention matrix, so carrying coldest-by-attention onto the fused path forces materializing it — the exact tax EpiKV names — whereas the epiphany score (a hidden-state delta) is forward-pass-readable without it; EpiKV is the fused-kernel-compatible importance signal for the gap #856 leaves on fak's fused path. **NOT adopted as a mechanism:** fak has an attention-mass observer but NO hidden-state-delta observer (the epiphany score's carrier), and proving any lossy eviction policy preserves answer quality on long reasoning traces needs a **served long-CoT eviction-quality benchmark this win32 dev box cannot run** (host-gated, [AVOID-TESTING-ON-THIS-MACHINE](docs/notes/AVOID-TESTING-ON-THIS-MACHINE-2026-06-25.md)). **Not a threat** (a performance capability, no adversary). **One residual, FILED not built:** a forward-pass hidden-state-delta observer feeding the kvmmu exact evictor (the representation-delta twin of `attn_observer`→#856), gated on that served witness. The one concrete artifact: a §B2 catalog row in [`docs/awesome-token-efficiency.md`](docs/awesome-token-efficiency.md). No capability code change; no change to `tools/idea_scout.py` (surfaced + scored correctly under topic `kv-prefix-cache-reuse`, score 44, on-topic and close-to-mission).
- [idea-scout triage: Pipelock / agent egress firewall with signed action receipts (#1267) (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-pipelock-agent-egress-receipts-triage-2026-06-29.md) — triage of GitHub repo `luckyPipewrench/pipelock`: a Go agent-egress firewall that mediates HTTP/WebSocket/CONNECT/MCP/A2A traffic, scans for secret exfiltration / SSRF / prompt injection, and emits mediator-signed action receipts. Verdict: **prior art to cite + same-side sibling control** — it independently validates fak's mediator-outside-the-agent boundary and witness-not-self-report stance, especially external evidence from the mediator; scanner/proxy mechanisms are **not adopted** because fak's load-bearing floor remains structured tool-call adjudication, IFC sink gating, and result admission, with detectors deliberately additive. **Named residual:** fak's audit journal is hash-chained and tamper-evident, but not a pinned-signer action-receipt format; signed receipt export is a future audit-evidence follow-on, not shipped here. No change to `tools/idea_scout.py`.
- [idea-scout triage: FuzzingLabs/mcp-security-hub — offensive-security MCP tooling (#1265) (2026-06-29)](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-security-hub-offensive-tooling-triage-2026-06-29.md) — triage of GitHub repo `FuzzingLabs/mcp-security-hub` (595 stars, MIT): a catalog of **38 Dockerized MCP servers across 14 security domains** that expose offensive-security tools to AI assistants — recon (Nmap, Masscan, Shodan), web exploitation (Nuclei, SQLMap, Nikto, Ffuf, Burp), binary RE (Radare2, Ghidra, IDA Pro), credential cracking (Hashcat), AD pathing (BloodHound), exploit lookup (Searchsploit), each wired in through `claude_desktop_config.json`. Verdict: **a concrete dual-use THREAT-SURFACE EXEMPLAR + prior art to cite; mechanism NOT adopted** — it is the first idea-scout candidate that is a real, popular, attach-in-minutes inventory of high-capability offensive tools rather than a paper, i.e. exactly the tool inventory fak's **default-deny capability floor** exists to govern. Each tool reaches fak as a structured tool call; none matches the guard default policy's allowlist or its read-only allow-prefixes (`read_`/`get_`/`search_`/`list_`/`lookup_`/`find_`), so each stays **`DEFAULT_DENY`** at the kernel (`cmd/fak/attest.go:237`) until an operator authorizes it — the **enforced** form of the repo's own README process (obtain written authorization, define scope, maintain audit logs), turned from prose into a capability policy + a hash-chained decision journal, with offensive calls further mapping onto fak's IFC sink classes (Exec/Egress/Destructive). **Honest fence:** fak denies these because they are not allow-listed, **not** because it "knows" Nmap is offensive ([detector-is-not-the-floor](docs/notes/RESEARCH-defensive-misdirection-triage-2026-06-23.md)) — so fak ships **no** curated offensive-tooling starter policy and **no** adjudicator test corpus drawn from this catalog; the smallest honest follow-on is that fixture/corpus, **FILED not built**. **Adopt?** No — fak is the governor, not the offensive toolbox. **Defend against?** Indirectly — the threat is an agent with these tools attached and invoked without authorization/scope; the default-deny floor + journal + sink-gating is the containment. **Cite as prior art?** Yes, as a dual-use threat-surface catalog, never as a fak mechanism — the offensive-MCP sibling of the defensive-MCP triages [#552](docs/notes/RESEARCH-mcp-privacy-leakage-triage-2026-06-23.md) / [#911](docs/notes/RESEARCH-sharelock-multitool-threshold-poisoning-triage-2026-06-26.md) / [#1267](docs/notes/RESEARCH-pipelock-agent-egress-receipts-triage-2026-06-29.md). No change to `tools/idea_scout.py` (surfaced + scored correctly, topic `mcp-security`, score 40).

Add is one command (`fak accounts add <name>`); retire is one command (`fak accounts remove --name <seat> --archive` — tombstone + `.DELETED-<date>` dir-rename + registry repoint + resync, refusing the live `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` seat). Plus three systemic moves beyond one-off retirement — a `dedup` skill that acts via the `modularize` code-motion recipe, generator-extraction tickets to kill clones at the source (verified: `ParseLedger` ×5 packages; `gradeFor` ×34 shown to be a token-window mirage over 2 differently-signatured defs), and making the sub-category thresholds themselves ratchetable via score-2x. - [The shared-trunk compile-integrity gap: lease disjointness is not build isolation (2026-07-06)](docs/notes/SHARED-TRUNK-COMPILE-INTEGRITY-GAP-2026-07-06.md) — a witnessed incident (a peer's uncommitted call to an undefined `runSuperloopDrive` broke every session's `cmd/fak` build) exposes a fourth axis the git-admin epic's three named gaps (#822/#834) do not cover. Disjoint file leases (`dos arbitrate`) guarantee ownership disjointness at *file* granularity; Go compiles at *package* granularity, so two agents with disjoint leases in one package still share one build graph — and on a shared dirty tree an agent cannot witness its own change, the one place the witness-not-self-report floor cannot stand. Shows neither `pre-commit` (7 content gates) nor `pre-push` (`dos review` + tier drift) compiles anything, so the runbook's archive-HEAD build stays manual. Proposes raising *atomic commit* from "coherent file set" to "coherent file set proven to compile against HEAD in isolation": a static self-containment rung at the commit seam (`COMMIT_NOT_SELF_CONTAINED`, no build) and an affected-scoped isolated build rung at the push seam (`TRUNK_WOULD_NOT_COMPILE`), both fail-closed with the standard block|warn|off ladder, reusing `affectedtests.Select` and the archive-HEAD technique. Design only; the hook change is park-by-default/sensitive and wants its own issue + warn-soak.
- [RSI market-discovery epic: file a ticket the moment the market moves (2026-07-08)](docs/notes/rsi-market-discovery-epic-2026-07-08.md) — a reviewable seed of 12 broad research tickets for the self-improving work-discovery loop, anchored on the just-shipped arXiv+GitHub+**Hacker News** `idea-scout` spine (HN via the public Algolia `search_by_date` API, newest-first — a trending front-page story becomes a candidate within moments of release). The tickets span five axes: fresh market-condition crawl (Reddit/X/new-release-firehose adapters, cross-source deep dedup + a durable seen-everything index), a minimum spine (a `Source` interface + conformance suite so new sources are cheap), domain-specific support (per-vertical topic packs + a "scout my domain" onramp for outside agent builders), operator-steerable superloops (a scheduled discovery loop + a super-meta loop that reweights topics from triage outcomes, and a single steer surface to pause/reweight/re-scope from above), and the honesty surface (concept-disambiguation of discovered ideas + an AEO explainer and a QA gate that every auto-filed issue body stays honest). Each ticket names a concrete first deliverable and a witness; only the HN/arXiv/GitHub spine is shipped, everything else is open research/build.
- [Dispatch-worker context-budget trap: the derived-budget fix and its measured-baseline residual (2026-07-08)](docs/notes/dispatch-worker-context-budget-trap-2026-07-08.md) — the tracking note commit `8a0fcffbb` referenced but never created. Records the #2972 crash-loop where the claude dispatch worker's guard `--context-budget-tokens` (a flat 48000) sat below the workers' ~62K irreducible turn-1 baseline prompt, so every worker was born over-budget and drained `BUDGET_CONTEXT_EXHAUSTED` on turn 1 (`DebitUsage` debits the FULL resident window each turn) → restarts burned → `CHILD_CRASH`, fleet ship rate 2–9%. Documents the shipped derived-budget fix (`min(claudeGuardBaselineTokens*2, HardContextCap−OutputReserve)` = `min(62000*2, 200000−32000)` = 124000, golden-pinned on the Go + Python sides) and the #3522 follow-up that turned the frozen 62000 baseline into a **floored, raising-only measurement**: `baseline = max(Σ (bytes+3)/4 over the launch constituents readable at launch — AGENTS/llms/CLAUDE/MEMORY + the `route` blob when named via `DISPATCH_STARTUP_BUNDLE`, 62000)`. Because it floors AT the old constant and only ever raises, it closes the organic-orientation-growth hole without the false-confidence trap of a static repo-file sum that *asserts* the fraction bounds the whole (the rejected non-fix) — the floor stays authoritative for every dynamic term the sum can't see. Emits `guard_baseline_tokens`/`guard_context_budget_tokens` as an observable. Residual still open: the per-issue body + Claude Code system prompt (and the `route` blob absent the env, and the injected fleet memory which really lives in the per-project claude memory dir, not a workspace-root MEMORY.md file) are floor-covered only, so runtime attribution — a turn-1 drain emitting a distinct `real_baseline>budget` reason instead of a generic crash — is the highest-preference of the remaining checkable steps.
- [Worktree land merge-reconciliation contract: a rejected apply is a structured refusal, never a silent drop (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/WORKTREE-LAND-MERGE-RECONCILIATION-2026-07-10.md) — the #1334 land seam's all-or-nothing `git apply` rejects whenever the trunk moved inside the worker's diff region since its pinned base, and the old contract failed open with generic prose — silently dropping the worker's entire already-verified delta, the one outcome the witness-not-self-report floor can never audit. Compares three reconcile algorithms (rebase-before-every-land, `git apply --3way`, serialized-apply-under-lane-lease-with-auto-rebase-on-reject) and picks the third: the fast path stays a plain apply, and conflicts only ever materialize inside the worker's isolated worktree — never as conflict markers on the shared trunk (`--3way`'s disqualifying failure mode). Names the landing session as the deterministic conflict owner (auto-rebase in place; STOP-and-replan on a genuine overlapping-region conflict — never a human merge queue, never a silent drop) and ships the contract half: `land_worktree_diff`'s reject arm now returns a structured refusal (`reason=COLLISION_RISK` from the closed refusal vocabulary — `dos man wedge COLLISION_RISK --explain`: refusal, route-to-replan — plus the git evidence and a replan `next_action`), doctest-pinned, with the STOP invariants (nothing applied, nothing committed, trunk clean, worktree kept as evidence). Composes with the compile-integrity rungs: a clean apply proves *textual* disjointness, not *semantic* compatibility, so the rebase arm re-runs the compile witness in the re-pinned worktree and the push-seam `TRUNK_WOULD_NOT_COMPILE` rung backstops the clean-apply-onto-a-moved-trunk case.
- [tair-kvcache borrow study: one cache-budget-sweep gap filed, the rest present-on-witness (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/tair-kvcache-borrow-study-2026-07-10.md) — a deep-read of [`alibaba/tair-kvcache`](https://github.com/alibaba/tair-kvcache) @ `6896aae1` (Apache-2.0) scanned for cache/latency techniques fak lacks, borrow-not-vendor, with every candidate **witnessed against the actual tree** before filing. Net result: one genuine high-leverage borrow filed — [#3952](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3952), a single-pass cache-budget→reuse miss-ratio-curve sweep (replay a prefix-access trace over N budgets **plus one unbounded pass** → reuse-vs-budget curve + infinite-cache **theoretical ceiling** + the smallest budget at 99% of it, the ROI **knee**) to size `--compact-history-budget` and the radixkv LRU budget from evidence instead of intuition, reusing `radixkv` as the replay engine and `cacheobs`/`cachevalueledger` as the realized-reuse feeders (upstream `optimizer/analysis/script/run/tradeoff.py` + `manager/optimizer_runner.cc`). The other candidates were **dropped on witness** as already present, the redundancy the witness step exists to catch: the calibration + MAPE-vs-measured gate is present ×3 (`gateway/calibration_corpus_drift_test.go` fails CI on const-vs-corpus drift; `resume_projection.go` exposes OBSERVED−PROJECTED residual gauges + a refit signal; `vcachecal` reports false-warm/false-cold rather than assuming zero) — its only absent remainder is an XGBoost residual model fak's deterministic-auditable ethos would reject; gen-fenced stale-lease reclaim is `leaseref/fence.go` (monotonic fencing token, CAS, halt-and-reacquire on `STALE_LEASE`); single-flight is subsumed by `callavoid`'s break-even + amplification economics; LeafAwareLRU/tiered-demote/quota map to `radixkv`/`cachemeta`/headroom. Fixed-vs-sliding-window TTL deferred (low-leverage; `cachemeta` already separates `AdmittedAt` from `LastAccess`).
- [KernelWiki borrow study: 5 sub-epics + 28 leaves onto existing seams, 47 candidates witnessed (2026-07-10)](docs/notes/2026-07-10-kernelwiki-study.md) — a deep `/study-repo` pass over [`mit-han-lab/KernelWiki`](https://github.com/mit-han-lab/KernelWiki) @ `76d27b56` (a curated GPU-kernel knowledge base + CI-invariant ingestion pipeline; tooling MIT-style, all borrows **inspire**, no bytes vendored), scanned for the corpus-hygiene disciplines fak's knowledge/claim/ingest surfaces lack. 47 grounded candidates, one capability-witness each **dogfooding fak's own self-index**: **43 PARTIAL, 3 ABSENT, 1 PRESENT**. The signal is not "fak lacks a feature" but "fak has the seam — `freshness.go` DriftKinds, `CLAIMS.md`/`honesty.go:30` one-tag lint, `idea_scout` firehose, `benchauthority` — without the invariant (closed vocab, bidirectional registry, reproducibility floor, committed decision ledger, byte-checkable provenance)." The fileable candidates organize onto **five existing fak seams**, filed as **five single-axis sub-epics** under umbrella epic [#3946](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3946), each **fully decomposed into independently-shippable child leaves** (28 leaves, #4072–#4099): committed decision-ledger + skip-audit closure over `idea_scout.py:445` ([#3947](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3947), 5 leaves); frontmatter-derived regenerated indices + internal citation-graph gate over `internal/devindex/freshness.go` ([#3948](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3948), 7 leaves); evidence-tiered VERIFIED gate + machine-witnessed reproducibility floor over `honesty.go`/`benchauthority` ([#3949](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3949), 6 leaves); version-sensitive-claim registry + cutoff SoT + offline tool-version time-decay ([#3950](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3950), 6 leaves); immutable source-page layer + typed provenance taxonomy + drift re-check generalizing `swebenchsota.go:79` ([#3951](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3951), 4 leaves). Two candidates **deduped into already-open issues** (alias-aware retrieval → [#3925](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3925); object-reuse eval-cache rung → [#3923](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/3923)); **8 Blackwell decode-kernel borrows captured as a file-after-measurement shortlist** (3 ABSENT) rather than filed speculatively; 1 dropped PRESENT. Full 47-candidate ledger with per-candidate verdict + KernelWiki source anchor + fak seam + leaf number, plus the completeness critic's unopened-area disclosure, inside.
- [Decode occupancy/traffic witness landed + A100 ncu measurement gate (2026-07-11)](docs/notes/2026-07-11-decode-occupancy-witness-measurement.md) — the follow-on that discharges the [KernelWiki study](docs/notes/2026-07-10-kernelwiki-study.md)'s **file-after-measurement** gate on its Blackwell decode-kernel shortlist (Cluster G, 8). Lands `internal/compute/decode_occupancy.go` (7 tests green, on origin/main) + its A100 Nsight-Compute harness `tools/dgx_decode_occupancy_ncu.sh`. The file/no-file verdict is **exact** (grid-block counts vs SM counts, operand bytes): flash decode launches one block per query head, so SmolLM2's `nH=9` grid leaves **99 of 108 A100 SMs idle every step** — register-independent — and decode attention is memory-bound at ~0.5 FLOP/byte. Verdict: **4 FILE** (persistent-kernel tail, `__ldcs` L1 hints, CLC tile-scheduling wave-quant tail, MoE launch-fusion), **1 no-gap** (tmem-accumulator-migration — grid-bound not per-SM-bound, the speculative ticket the gate stops), **3 defer** (nvfp4/PDL/CLC-try-cancel are sm_90+/sm_100-only, not A100-measurable). The device ncu corroboration (achieved-occupancy / DRAM-%) was dispatched but the Slack control-bridge readback was transport-wedged this session (`context deadline exceeded`; **no device numbers fabricated**); the note carries the one-command recipe to obtain it when the bridge is healthy.
- [DeepWiki concept study: a fak-native witness-verified repo wiki track — 1 epic + 7 leaves (2026-07-11)](docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-DEEPWIKI-2026-07-11.md) — a `/study-repo` pass over [`AsyncFuncAI/deepwiki-open`](https://github.com/AsyncFuncAI/deepwiki-open) @ `16f35a0` (MIT, borrows **inspire**, the open reimplementation of Cognition's hosted DeepWiki) + a SOTA scan of **CodeWiki** (arXiv:2510.24428, `CodeWikiBench`) and `open-deepwiki`, scoping the human/LLM-facing **navigable narrative wiki** fak lacks. Reads DeepWiki's two generation prompts (`src/app/[owner]/[repo]/page.tsx`): phase-1 emits a `<wiki_structure>` XML tree (8–12 pages over a fixed taxonomy, `:746-795,829`), phase-2 writes each page grounded **only** in its `relevant_files` with a forced ≥5-file `<details>` header and `Sources:[file:line]()` per claim (`:420-526`), retrieval by FAISS (`api/rag.py:41`). The decisive framing: every tool in the field shares **two weaknesses that are exactly fak's thesis** — (1) citations are LLM-produced and **never resolved against the tree** (`:497-502`; "reduce but do not eliminate hallucination"), and (2) **snapshot staleness** (regenerated on a schedule, "may lag main by hours to days"); meanwhile fak already holds as **ground truth** the structure DeepWiki re-infers with an LLM+FAISS pass (`internal/devindex`). So the fak wiki **seeds structure from the self-index and witnesses every citation** — the two things the field structurally cannot do. Filed as epic [#4277](https://github.com/anthony-chaudhary/fak/issues/4277) + 7 independently-shippable leaves (#4278–#4284): L1 `fak wiki structure` (deterministic tree from `devindex`, P1 foundation), L2 source-grounded page-gen, L3 witnessed `Sources:[path:line]` resolver (P1 anti-hallucination core), L4 freshness-gated pages (`DriftStaleWikiPage` over `freshness.go`), L5 Mermaid diagrams from the real `refs.go`/`blastradius` graph, L6 claims-aware stub/simulated banner from `CLAIMS.md`, L7 `fak wiki score` coverage/citation-resolve gate. Builds on the queryable-self-index epic [#1287]; **ablated against the sibling KernelWiki index-integrity track** [#3948] so L3/L4 cross-link (#4079/#4077) rather than dupe — that track hardens the *internal fact-index*, this one generates the *narrative wiki*. Nothing built; every borrow is `path:line@16f35a0`-anchored and witnessed PARTIAL/ABSENT before filing.
- [In-flight repair of a sanctioned compiled sidestep: auto-substitute `git push` → `fak sync push` (2026-07-15)](docs/notes/IN-FLIGHT-REPAIR-SANCTIONED-SIDESTEP-2026-07-15.md) — answers "if `fak sync push` is such an obvious sanctioned equivalent, why doesn't the guard repair the refused `git push` in flight?" The rewrite-and-execute mechanism already exists — the `VerdictTransform{NewArgs}` verdict applied at `internal/gateway/adjudicate_proposed.go:446-454` — but is scoped to same-tool **argument** edits (secret redaction, `attenuateCLIGrammar`, `_fak_confirm` strip); the `git push → fak sync push` sidestep is only ever surfaced as advisory prose (`reversibilityFamily.hint` → `Detail["remedy"]` → `remedyNote`), and `#4428` "error affordance rewrites" rewrites note **text**, not calls. Designs a conservative, **opt-in** (`FAK_GUARD_AUTOREPAIR=sidestep`) auto-substitution: fire only when the family carries a `fak ` compiled sidestep (`compiledSidestepRemedy`), the call is a **Bash** command (a pure `NewArgs` rewrite — no ABI `NewTool` needed, unlike the MCP-tool variant), and the command is in the **safe-equivalent subset** (bare `git push` with none of `--force`/`--delete`/`--tags`/`--all`/`--mirror`/`--prune`/explicit refspec — structurally decidable via the existing `gitDryRunPreview` flag parser). Sound because `SafePush` pushes the **current branch** non-force ff-only, so bare `git push` ≈ `fak sync push`; the pause is theater for the safe subset and load-bearing only for the dangerous variants (which stay a `RequireWitness` pause — auto-laundering `--force` into a non-force verb would silently change intent). Implementation is **core-locked** (`internal/adjudicator`, `corelockaudit.go:64`) → needs `fak commit --core-lock-maintenance-witness`; the gateway/env wiring is not. Suggests 4 increments (promote `familyRemedyCommands` out of `_test` → substitute verdict in the rung → gateway/env wiring → extend to issue-create/slack/messaging families). Design only; nothing built.

- [Downstream fak-adopter review: 15 public-safe imports + 10 measured substrate gaps (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/DOWNSTREAM-FAK-ADOPTION-REVIEW-2026-08-05.md) — a review of a contract-delivered Go repository that adopted fak's guard/lane/worktree/shipgate concepts and hardened them against a ~30-session shared checkout and a 50-150 worker agent fleet, extracted with the client/domain filter stated in the note. The ranked imports lead with four variations on one question fak's tooling does not ask — *what did this gate quantify over, and can it still be shown to fire*: `null`-not-`0` for unmeasured KPIs, candidate-count printing so an empty domain is never spelled `clean`, a selftest that proves each probe fires (`probes_trusted`), and a FLOOR/objective split so a shipgate loop cannot buy score by deleting the thing its metric counts. Plus a measured worker-preamble cost model (the fixed prefix is ~39% of a worker's `cache_read`; 68% of a wave's bill is context vs 31% production) and an ordinal landing deadline (26.9% of workers land nothing ever; the average refusal-death at turn 29 precedes the median first landing at turn 39). Ten measured defects in the fak/DOS lane substrate, each flagged for re-verification; one is half-confirmed at HEAD (`fak orient` reads `refs/fak/locks` while DOS journals to `.dos/lane-journal.jsonl` — two lease substrates, neither reader sees the other's holders) and one contradicts a standing memory note on judging lease liveness by pid. ⚠️ **Partially corrected** by the verification pass below — three of the four leading imports already exist in fak's scorecard fold.
- [Verifying the downstream-adopter review: three gaps that were not gaps (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/DOWNSTREAM-REVIEW-VERIFIED-2026-08-05.md) — runs the first three "next checkable steps" of the review above and reports what they returned. `tools/scorecard_control_pane.py` already sets `debt: None` for an unmeasured card, excludes it from `total_debt` **by type** rather than by value, prints `(N measured, M errored)`, **hard-fails the ratchet on any errored card**, and returns exit `2` when unpinned — so the `null`-not-`0`, could-not-run and floor recommendations were assessed against the scorecard producers and the pinned baseline without reading the fold that consumes them. What survives unifies two rules that looked separate: **both ratchet axes read the numerator and neither reads the domain**, so a card that goes *quiet* and a card whose counted corpus was *deleted* both arrive wearing an improvement — with 24 of 44 pinned metrics already at exactly `0`. Also fixes a real defect the adopter's config exposed and fak's own tree cannot reach: a multi-line `[lanes.trees]` array left the lane **declared but tree-less**, so `LaneForPath` returned `unknown` while every "is this lane real?" check passed (`internal/devindex/devindex.go`, pinned by `lanes_multiline_test.go`). Settles the lease-liveness contradiction with the journal — one holder journals up to three distinct pids, so the pid is the per-invocation CLI child and never a liveness signal, while `proc_starttime` is recorded on 60/60 leases and read by nothing; 18 of 60 leases were never released and `ttl_minutes` is `null` on every one. Closes on the method rule the miss earned: **an absence claim names the files it searched.**
- [What a downstream contract deployment taught fak — mined, verified, and mostly refuted (2026-08-05)](docs/notes/DOWNSTREAM-ADOPTION-MINED-LEARNINGS-2026-08-05.md) — the mining pass that the two notes above were a correction to, run end to end and then handed to an adversarial refute stage. Seven areas of a private downstream fak deployment yielded **125 candidate mechanisms**; three areas reached verification and refutation, and there **27 of 41 candidates were refuted — including the load-bearing gap claim in 3 of 3 areas**, every time because the miner searched for the downstream's vocabulary and missed fak's word for the same shipped mechanism (`servewiring.go`'s wiring-verdict table, `gitgate`'s deny-as-value, `leaseref`'s CAS + TTL + liveness, `laneadmit`'s lane-name refusal axis, the block-mode `CONCEPT_ADMISSION` gate, "has teeth" as the must-NOT-fire control). Two independent passes the same day made the identical error, so it is a property of the method: **fak's substrate is further ahead of its own documentation than of its downstream, and the cheapest fix is a name, not a mechanism.** Fourteen imports survive, led by a verb-count KPI that reads one of `cmd/fak/main.go`'s two dispatch switches and a `--help` that exits 2 against fak's own written rule. Fifteen fak defects fall out, **six re-derived independently here** — among them `kpi_anchored` grading on file existence alone, so all 403 rows anchoring the concept glossary score `crystal` while 167 name a concept that page never mentions, and two disjoint reason sets (19 vs 75, zero overlap) that two docs each call "the closed vocabulary." ⚠️ Four of the seven areas never reached verification (three upstream errors, one stalled on all six attempts); their 84 candidates are unchecked and are excluded from every finding.

## Research notes — 2026-08-13

- [`docs/notes/NATIVE-HARNESS-CORE-READINESS-2026-08-13.md`](docs/notes/NATIVE-HARNESS-CORE-READINESS-2026-08-13.md) — core-readiness update on the fak-owned agent loop, superseding the two stale June notes. Streaming and the durable turn checkpoint (the June "Crush is ahead" gaps) both **shipped**, along with structured progress SSE, the mid-flight verb mailbox, terminal-tool loop wake, and per-call account routing. The gap the June map missed: **the owned loop has no work surface** — `internal/agent/loop.go:401` wires it to the travel-demo `ToolCatalog()` over `kernel.New("localtools")`, and `internal/gateway/native_serve.go:216` seeds it with `lastUserText(req.Messages)` alone, so the served request's `tools[]` and conversation history are dropped. `fak serve --native` therefore cannot serve a real client conversation and `fak chat` cannot edit a file; #1380's DoD run has been unwitnessed since June for exactly that reason. Maps the twelve (all OPEN) children of the harness-native program #2387, the six rungs of today's resource-governance epic #6552 — whose seat-pooling design has the demo fixture as its subject until the tool surface generalizes — and argues the Claude Code import worth making is the **tool contract and its cross-call invariants** (read-before-edit, exact-match uniqueness, truncation ceilings) as kernel contracts, plus canonicalization-before-matching (#2407), *not* its permission regexes, which fak's adjudicator (`cli_grammar`/`shell_dialect`/`rce_pipe`/`argcanon`) already beats.
- [`docs/notes/AGENTQUERY-INDEX-DECISION-2026-08-19.md`](docs/notes/AGENTQUERY-INDEX-DECISION-2026-08-19.md) — Cross-platform bounded-query benchmark and evidence-based no-index threshold.
- [`docs/notes/OTLP-ZERO-DEPENDENCY-DECISION-2026-08-19.md`](docs/notes/OTLP-ZERO-DEPENDENCY-DECISION-2026-08-19.md) — OTLP exporter dependency, privacy, cardinality, and adoption-threshold decision.
- [docs/notes/SESSION-HISTORY-REFRESH-SLO-2026-08-19.md](docs/notes/SESSION-HISTORY-REFRESH-SLO-2026-08-19.md) — Reproducible cold/warm/change-one session-history benchmark, default-on SLOs, and aggregate-only real-corpus witness.
- [docs/provider-cost-reconciliation.md](docs/provider-cost-reconciliation.md) — Authoritative provider billing-export import, exact session-to-root attribution, coverage, and reconciliation contract.

## Research notes — 2026-08-21

- [`docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-ULTRACODE-WORKFLOWS-2026-08-21.md`](docs/notes/CONCEPT-STUDY-ULTRACODE-WORKFLOWS-2026-08-21.md) — Pinned `/study-repo` + `/field-borrow` comparison of public Claude Code workflow/agent examples, LangGraph, stable A2A, black-box installed Claude behavior, and fak's Ultracode baseline across twelve orchestration axes. Keeps leases and independent effect witness as fak's stronger defaults, deduplicates the existing issue cluster, records effective activation under #8488, and files the sole ownerless recovery join as #8490. A machine companion and deterministic Go checker enforce provenance, license, posture, ownership, benchmark denominators/falsifiers, completeness, and public-leak boundaries. The #8997 refresh adds the pinned exhaustive A2A tree/non-tree denominator, cutoff-filtered issue/PR evidence, explicit discussion-history limits, FAK self-query decisions, and binding follow-ons.
